### User

_source line 3584 · 2026-05-23T08:48:04.920080+00:00_

[Director 指令] ## 论文审计修复：2 CRITICAL + 关键 WARNING

### CRITICAL 1: Seed 计数不一致（27% → 36%）

Table 2 有 4 个 G 类种子（seed1†, seed4, seed5, seed6），但正文 5 处写 "27% (3/11)"。seed1 是 G† 但被排除在计数外，没给理由。

**修复**：全文统一为 **4/11 = 36%** generalized（包含 seed1）。seed1 的 eval protocol 差异已在 Table 2 脚注标注。

需要修改的 5 处：
1. `main.tex` abstract: "only 27% achieve" → "only 36% achieve"，"3/11" 相关数字更新
2. `introduction.tex` contribution 3: "27%" → "36%"
3. `experiments.tex:101` 附近: "3/11 generalize (27%)" → "4/11 generalize (36%)"
4. `conclusion.tex`: "only 27% generalize" → "only 36% generalize"
5. `main.tex` Limitations: 如有 27% → 36%

同时检查 "73% avoid collapse" 是否仍正确——Collapsed 是 3/11 = 27%，Non-collapsed 是 8/11 = 73%，✓ 不变。

### CRITICAL 2: Cell C 定义矛盾

**问题**：`method.tex:53` 说 Cell C 是"degenerate"（full observation with one window makes step- and episode-level advantage identical），但 `experiments.tex:14` 和 `appendix.tex:98-100` 描述 Cell C 为非退化配置（full context visibility + step-level advantage normalization），尝试了 6 次但 crash。

**根本原因**：Method 中 "full observation" = 单窗口 = 退化；Experiments 中 "full observation" = 全上下文可见 + 多步 advantage（每步单独计算 advantage，不是整个 episode 一个 advantage）。

**修复**：修改 `method.tex:53` 的 factorial 描述，明确 Cell C 的定义，删除 "degenerate" 表述：

将 "The fourth cell is degenerate (full observation with one window makes step- and episode-level advantage identical)." 

替换为：

"The fourth cell---\textbf{Cell~C} (full trajectory + step-level advantage; only M2 active)---would isolate sign misattribution without information loss. We attempted this configuration but encountered persistent training instability (Section~\ref{sec:factorial_experiments}; Appendix~\ref{app:cell_c})."

### WARNING 1: Table 1 缺 SFT OOD baseline

`experiments.tex` Table 1 的 SFT baseline 行 OOD 列是 "---"，但 Table 2 明确给出 SFT OOD = 6.72%。

**修复**：Table 1 SFT baseline 的 OOD 列从 "---" 改为 "6.72"。

### WARNING 2: 缩写未展开

首次使用处展开以下 3 个缩写：
- **OOD**: 在 abstract 或 intro 首次出现时展开为 "out-of-distribution (OOD)"
- **SFT**: 首次出现时展开为 "supervised fine-tuning (SFT)"  
- **FSDP**: experiments.tex 中首次出现时展开为 "Fully Sharded Data Parallel (FSDP)"

### WARNING 3: GPU/计算资源信息

在 `appendix.tex` 超参数表后添加一段硬件信息：
- GPU 型号：A100 80GB（或实际使用的型号，检查训练配置）
- 单次实验训练时间（约多少小时）
- 总 GPU-hours（粗略估算即可）

如果不确定具体型号，检查 bjb1 服务器的 GPU 信息或训练 log。

### WARNING 4: Em-dash 去 AI 风格

全文搜索 "---" 的解释性用法（不包括 Table 中的缺失值标记），将以下模式替换为逗号、括号或拆句：
- "pattern---decomposing" → "pattern that decomposes" 或用逗号
- "---sign errors cause" → "; sign errors cause" 或拆句
- "---constant ceiling versus cumulative degradation---" → "(constant ceiling versus cumulative degradation)"

至少处理正文中最明显的 5-6 处，不需要改全部 25 处（appendix 的可以保留）。

---

修完后编译确认，把 CRITICAL 修改发给我。

_source line 3588 · 2026-05-23T08:48:22.364043+00:00_

48	Episode-level training resolves both mechanisms by treating the complete trajectory as a single GRPO sample. The full history $h_t$ is available at every step (eliminating M1), and a single advantage $\hat{A}$ applies uniformly to all tokens (eliminating M2). Uniform reinforcement of successful trajectories preserves good sub-strategies; window-level sign errors actively destroy them. This is GRPO's original design---step-independent windowing is the deviation.
49	
50	\subsection{Factorial Experiment Design}
51	\label{sec:factorial_design}
52	
53	To separate the two mechanisms, we implement a $2 \times 2$ factorial varying observation scope and advantage granularity: \textbf{Cell~A} (truncated $h{=}2$ + step-level advantage; both mechanisms active), \textbf{Cell~B} (truncated $h{=}2$ + episode-level advantage; only M1 active), and \textbf{Cell~D} (full trajectory + episode-level advantage; neither active). The fourth cell is degenerate (full observation with one window makes step- and episode-level advantage identical). Comparing A vs.\ B isolates sign misattribution; comparing B vs.\ D isolates information loss.
54

17	\title{Sign Misattribution in Step-Independent Agent RL:\\Dual Mechanism Diagnosis and Episode-Level Fix}
18	
19	\author{Anonymous}

21	\begin{document}
22	\maketitle
23	
24	\begin{abstract}
25	Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning requires credit assignment across long interaction trajectories, yet current frameworks default to step-independent windowing that decomposes trajectories into independently optimized windows. This introduces a structural credit assignment pathology driven by two mechanisms: \textit{policy information loss}, which truncates context and imposes a constant performance ceiling, and \textit{advantage sign misattribution}, which causes gradient updates to reflect episode outcomes rather than local action quality, producing cumulative degradation. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with group size $K{=}8$, theory predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign under binary reward; empirical measurement under penalty reward yields a 52\% credit agreement rate, indistinguishable from random chance. A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms: eliminating sign misattribution converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning; removing information loss enables generalization. Episode-level Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), treating the full trajectory as a single optimization sample, resolves both mechanisms. A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% of seeds avoid collapse, but only 27\% achieve statistically significant OOD generalization ($p{<}0.01$; 25--31\% OOD vs.\ 6.7\% SFT baseline). Preliminary single-seed

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Reinforcement learning from sparse terminal reward has emerged as a promising paradigm for training LLM agents on multi-turn interactive tasks spanning 30--50 interaction steps~\cite{kim2025reflact,digiRL2024,wang2022scienceworld}. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, with its critic-free design, has become the algorithm of choice. Yet a gap persists between the promise of RL-driven agent improvement and the reality of training outcomes: recent agent RL systems~\cite{ragen2025,selfguide2026,spear2025,feng2025gigpo} share a common, unscrutinized implementation pattern---decomposing trajectories into fixed-size windows, each optimized independently---and frequently report training instability without diagnosing its root cause.
5	
6	We show this \textit{step-independent} default introduces a fundamental credit assignment pathology. Step-independent training partitions trajectories into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each optimized via GRPO. This produces a two-phase collapse: task success drops to 0\% while action validity initially remains high, then continued training destroys even basic formatting. Standard remedies---dense reward shaping, discount tuning---fail. Concurrent methods that improve per-step credit quality (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}, GAGPO~\cite{gagpo2026}, Turn-PPO~\cite{turnppo2026}) address symptoms but not the structural root cause: the windowing itself.
7	
8	We trace the collapse to two mechanisms at different layers. \textit{\textbf{Policy information loss}} (M1) operates at the inference layer: each window lacks access to earlier observations, imposing a constant performance ceiling. \textit{\textbf{Advantage sign misattribution}} (M2) operates at the training layer: under binary reward, the advantage sign reflects episode success rather than local action quality. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally
…[truncated 1869 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Step-independent training in multi-turn agent RL fails through two mechanisms: policy information loss imposes a performance ceiling, while advantage sign misattribution causes cumulative degradation---at $p{=}12.5\%$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}$80.9\% incorrect-sign updates under binary reward (52\% measured under penalty reward, consistent with near-random credit assignment). A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms; episode-level GRPO resolves both (17.2\% mean OOD at 1.7B; preliminary single-seed 8B evidence shows 90.3\% OOD). A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% avoid collapse, but only 27\% generalize ($p{<}0.01$). The central insight---sign errors are more destructive than magnitude imprecision---applies to any agent RL method combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization.
5

100	
101	Three distinct outcomes emerge. 3/11 seeds collapse (27\%), with response length inflating to 14{,}000--16{,}000 tokens (Appendix~\ref{app:response_length}); 4/11 are non-collapsed but stagnant (36\%), with OOD indistinguishable from SFT (10.45--15.67\%, all NS at $\alpha{=}0.01$); and 3/11 generalize (27\%), reaching 25.37--30.60\% OOD ($p{<}0.001$). No collapsed seed generalizes, confirming stable training is necessary. One seed (seed2) shows delayed learning: 15.67\% OOD after 15 steps ($p{=}0.02$, borderline).% exp_id: exp029 multi-seed series
102	
103	The stagnant--generalized gap is not explained by insufficient training: seed100 improves only 1.50pp from step~5 to step~10 (11.19\% $\to$ 12.69\%, both NS), while seed5 reaches 25.37\% at step~10. Stable training is necessary but not sufficient for OOD generalization---the mechanism driving seed-dependent transfer remains an open question (Section~\ref{sec:discussion}).
104	
105	\subsection{Cross-Scale Verification}
106	\label{sec:cross_scale}
107	
108	A single-seed experiment on Qwen3-8B provides preliminary evidence that both failure and fix persist at scale (Table~\ref{tab:8b_results}). Cell~A collapses within two steps (28.1\% $\to$ 1.5\%). Cell~D reaches 96.9\% in-distribution and 90.3\% OOD at step~10, exceeding the SFT baseline (80.6\% OOD) by 9.7pp. Restricting to $h{=}2$ drops in-distribution success from 68.75\% to 31.25\% (37.5pp gap). Single-seed due to FSDP constraints; multi-seed verification is needed to confirm robustness.
109

_source line 3614 · 2026-05-23T08:49:25.982939+00:00_

36	\section*{Limitations}
37	
38	Our experiments span two environments (ALFWorld, ScienceWorld) and two scales (1.7B, 8B); additional environments and scales would strengthen generality. The 8B and ScienceWorld results report single-seed runs due to computational constraints. Among 11 seeds at 1.7B, only 27\% achieve statistically significant OOD generalization; we cannot rule out that stagnant seeds would eventually generalize with substantially more training steps, though seed100's minimal improvement from step~5 to step~10 suggests the bottleneck is not simply training duration. The mechanism driving seed-dependent transfer remains an open question. For very long episodes ($T > 100$) or tasks with many independent sub-goals, uniform episode-level advantage may limit efficiency even though it preserves direction correctness.

_source line 3618 · 2026-05-23T08:49:36.322018+00:00_

60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), indistinguishable from 50\% random chance. Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63	
64	\subsection{Episode-Level Training: Seed Variability}
65	\label{sec:seed_variability}
66	
67	Episode-level GRPO resolves the dual-mechanism failure, but training outcomes remain seed-dependent. We systematically evaluate $N{=}11$ random seeds under identical hyperparameters, revealing a $2{\times}2$ structure: \textit{training stability} (collapsed vs.\ non-collapsed) $\times$ \textit{OOD generalization} (stagnant vs.\ generalized).
68	
69	We define \textbf{collapsed} seeds as those where in-distribution success declines $>$50\% from peak and remains below baseline for $\geq$3 consecutive steps. \textbf{Non-collapsed} seeds are the complement. Among non-collapsed seeds, we further distinguish \textbf{generalized} (Fisher exact $p{<}0.01$ vs.\ SFT on the 134-task OOD set) from \textbf{stagnant} (not significant vs.\ SFT). Table~\ref{tab:seed_variability} reports results for each seed at its best-performing checkpoint.

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Problem Formulation and GRPO}
5	\label{sec:problem_formulation}
6	
7	We model the multi-turn agent as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) where the policy $\pi_\phi(a_t \mid h_t)$ generates actions conditioned on interaction history $h_t = (o_1, a_1, \ldots, o_t)$. Episodes of $T$ steps terminate with binary reward $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} samples $K$ rollouts per task and computes group-relative advantages:
8	\begin{align}
9	\hat{A}_k &= \frac{R_k - \mu}{\sigma + \epsilon}, \notag \\
10	\mu &= \frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K R_j, \quad \sigma = \sqrt{\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K (R_j - \mu)^2}
11	\label{eq:grpo_advantage}
12	\end{align}
13	GRPO is episode-level by design: each rollout receives one return and one advantage, with no inherent temporal discounting.
14	
15	\subsection{Step-Independent Windowing}
16	\label{sec:step_independent}
17	
18	Agent RL frameworks must reconcile GRPO's episode-level design with the memory constraints of long trajectories. RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} introduces \textbf{step-independent training}: the trajectory is partitioned into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each receiving a discounted return:
19	\begin{equation}
20	R_k = \gamma^{(N - k)} \cdot R_{\text{episode}}
21	\label{eq:step_return}
22	\end{equation}
23	where $N = \lceil T/h \rceil$ is the number of windows. Each window is optimized as an independent GRPO sample. This reduces sequence length from $O(T)$ to $O(h)$ and multiplies the effective batch size, but structurally alters credit assignment.
24	
25	\subsection{Dual Mechanism Analysis}
26	\label{sec:dual_mechanism}
27	
28	Two mechanisms jointly cause training failure under step-independent optimization.
29	
30	\subsubsection{Mechanism 1: Policy Information Loss}
31	\label{sec:m1_information_loss}
32	
33	Step-independent training truncates context at both inference and training time. The policy $\pi_\phi(a_t \mid h_{\text{window}_k})
…[truncated 2569 chars]

1	\appendix
2	
3	\section{Theoretical Analysis: Full Derivations}
4	\label{app:theory_full}
5	
6	\subsection{Why Sign Misattribution is the Core Mechanism}
7	\label{app:sign_fundamental}
8	
9	The two mechanisms identified in Section~\ref{sec:dual_mechanism} operate at different layers and produce qualitatively different failures. Policy information loss (Mechanism 1) operates at the inference layer, restricting the observable context and establishing a structural performance ceiling that is constant across training. Sign misattribution (Mechanism 2) operates at the training layer, corrupting gradient directions and progressively degrading performance below this ceiling.
10	
11	\textit{Proof of Proposition 1.} Define ``correct sign'' as follows: for a window containing locally beneficial actions, the correct sign of $\hat{A}_k$ is positive (reinforcement); for a window containing locally detrimental actions, the correct sign is negative (suppression). Under binary reward, the return for window $k$ is $R_k = \gamma^{(N-k)} \cdot R_{\text{episode}}$ (Equation~\ref{eq:step_return}). In a failed episode ($R_{\text{episode}} = 0$), all windows receive $R_k = 0$ regardless of their local action quality. If the group contains at least one successful rollout, the group mean $\mu > 0$, and all windows in the failed episode receive negative advantages after normalization via Equation~\ref{eq:grpo_advantage}. This includes windows where the agent took locally correct actions. Now consider a window with good local actions. In a successful episode (probability $p$), the window receives positive $\hat{A}_k$, which is the correct sign. In a failed episode (probability $1 - p$), the window receives negative $\hat{A}_k$ whenever $\mu > 0$, which is the wrong sign. The probability that $\mu = 0$ (all $K$ rollouts in the group failed) is $(1-p)^K$, which shrinks exponentially with $K$. As $K$ grows, nearly every failed episode produces a non-zero group mean, and the window receives the
…[truncated 15181 chars]

_source line 3628 · 2026-05-23T08:52:04.034955+00:00_

24	\begin{abstract}
25	Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning requires credit assignment across long interaction trajectories, yet current frameworks default to step-independent windowing that decomposes trajectories into independently optimized windows. This introduces a structural credit assignment pathology driven by two mechanisms: \textit{policy information loss}, which truncates context and imposes a constant performance ceiling, and \textit{advantage sign misattribution}, which causes gradient updates to reflect episode outcomes rather than local action quality, producing cumulative degradation. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with group size $K{=}8$, theory predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign under binary reward; empirical measurement under penalty reward yields a 52\% credit agreement rate, indistinguishable from random chance. A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms: eliminating sign misattribution converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning; removing information loss enables generalization. Episode-level Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), treating the full trajectory as a single optimization sample, resolves both mechanisms. A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% of seeds avoid collapse, but only 36\% achieve statistically significant OOD generalization ($p{<}0.01$; 24--31\% OOD vs.\ 6.7\% SFT baseline). Preliminary single-seed cross-scale verification at 8B shows both failure and fix (90.3\% OOD vs.\ collapse to 1.5\%), and cross-environment validation on ScienceWorld provides evidence the collapse extends beyond ALFWorld.
26	\end{abstract}

105	\subsection{Cross-Scale Verification}
106	\label{sec:cross_scale}
107	
108	A single-seed experiment on Qwen3-8B provides preliminary evidence that both failure and fix persist at scale (Table~\ref{tab:8b_results}). Cell~A collapses within two steps (28.1\% $\to$ 1.5\%). Cell~D reaches 96.9\% in-distribution and 90.3\% OOD at step~10, exceeding the SFT baseline (80.6\% OOD) by 9.7pp. Restricting to $h{=}2$ drops in-distribution success from 68.75\% to 31.25\% (37.5pp gap). Single-seed due to FSDP constraints; multi-seed verification is needed to confirm robustness.
109

_source line 3642 · 2026-05-23T08:52:59.201281+00:00_

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Generality and mechanism interaction.}
5	Sign misattribution arises whenever three structural properties co-occur: (a) step-level windowing, (b) sparse binary reward, and (c) group-relative normalization. Under these conditions, the advantage sign collapses to a proxy for episode outcome regardless of local quality, and severity scales with task difficulty ($P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) \to p$). M1 and M2 interact in a vicious cycle: M1 lowers $p$ by preventing multi-step strategies, increasing wrong-sign updates under M2, which further degrades $p$---explaining why step-independent training produces active collapse rather than slow convergence.
6	
7	\paragraph{Why step-independent became the default.}
8	Six contributing factors---from context length constraints to framework design inertia---are detailed in Appendix~\ref{app:why_defaults}.
9	
10	\paragraph{Seed-dependent OOD generalization.}
11	The multi-seed evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:seed_variability}) reveals a bimodal distribution: a stagnant cluster (10--16\% OOD, NS vs.\ SFT) and a generalized cluster (25--31\%, $p{<}0.001$). Training duration does not explain the gap (seed100: $+$1.50pp from step~5 to~10; seed5: 25.37\% at step~10), pointing to early training dynamics or initialization sensitivity. Identifying this mechanism is an important open question.
12	
13	\paragraph{Practitioner guidance.}
14	Use episode-level credit when the full episode fits in context; otherwise use the largest feasible $h$ ($h{\geq}20$; $h{\leq}10$ collapses). Finer-grained methods (Turn-PPO, GAGPO) should be applied only after establishing an episode-level baseline, as they assume correct gradient direction.
15	
16

## Round 313

### User

_source line 3666 · 2026-05-23T09:26:26.929414+00:00_

[Director 指令] [Director 指令] Round 2 审稿修复 — Action Matrix（含 1 个实验 + 写作修复）

Deadline: 2026-05-25。分两批：先跑实验 + 关键写作，再根据实验结果更新主文。

---

## 批次 1：实验 + 关键写作（并行）

### 实验 A：纯 Binary Reward Credit Agreement 测量 [最高优先级]
**目的**：验证 Proposition 2 理论预测（19.1% correct sign）。当前 80.9% vs 52% 的 gap 是两轮审稿复发问题。
**配置**：
- Cell A 配置（h=2 step-independent），但 invalid_action_penalty = 0（纯 binary reward）
- 不需要训到收敛，只需 2-3 training steps 获得足够 rollout 数据
- 从 rollout 数据提取 advantage sign，计算 credit agreement rate（与 Section 4.4 同方法）
- 服务器 bjb1，cuda:0
- 预估 1-2 GPU-hours

**产出**：credit agreement rate + 样本量 + 95% CI。理想结果 ≈19-20%，直接验证理论。

### 实验 B：52.07% Binomial Test [分钟级]
**目的**：补充 Section 4.4 的统计检验（审稿 SF5）。
- 从现有 penalty reward 实验日志提取：样本量 n、credit agreement 比例
- 计算 binomial test p-value（H0: P=0.5）和 Wilson 95% CI
- 同时计算 H0: P=0.191（检验是否显著偏离 binary 理论值）

### 实验 C：Advantage 量级分布提取 [如果训练日志可用]
**目的**：验证 Eq.6 假设（审稿 MF2）。
- 从 Cell A (h=2) 训练日志提取 advantage 值
- 分组：correct sign (successful episode windows) vs incorrect sign (failed episode windows)
- 计算 |A_correct| / |A_wrong| 均值比
- GRPO normalization 理论预测比值 ≈ K-1 = 7，请验证实际数据是否一致

---

### 写作修复（与实验并行，不等实验结果）

#### W1 [MF2, CRITICAL]: Eq.6 cumulative divergence 重写
**位置**：theoretical_analysis.tex，Eq.6 (biased_gradient) 相关段落
**问题**：Reviewer 指出 "comparable magnitude" 假设错误。GRPO normalization 下 |A_correct| ≈ (K-1)|A_wrong|，期望梯度实际是弱正方向，"monotonic divergence" 不成立。
**修改**：
- 删除 "monotonic divergence" 声明和线性 bound（||θ_t - θ*|| ≥ ... 部分）
- 删除 "comparable magnitude" 假设
- 替换为以下论证（用你的判断润色成学术语言）：
  "GRPO normalization creates an asymmetry: correct-sign advantages have magnitude approximately $(K{-}1)$ times larger than incorrect-sign advantages. While this asymmetry makes the expected gradient weakly positive, 80.9\% of updates point in the wrong direction, creating extreme gradient variance. In practice, this variance-dominated regime prevents reliable convergence. Moreover, the M1--M2 positive feedback loop amplifies the problem: information loss (M1) caps the success rate $p$, lower $p$ increases the wrong-sign frequency under M2, which further depresses $p$. This vicious cycle drives the empirically observed collapse despite the weakly favorable gradient expectation."
- 如果有 simplifying assumption caveat（"Under the simplifying assumption..."），保留但修改为更准确的表述

#### W2 [MF5, CRITICAL]: Title + Claim 语言校准
**Title**：`Sign Misattribution in Step-Independent Agent RL: Dual Mechanism Diagnosis and Episode-Level Fix`
→ 改为 `Sign Misattribution in Step-Independent Agent RL: Dual Mechanism Diagnosis and Episode-Level Correction`

**Abstract**：找到 "resolves both mechanisms" → 改为 "addresses both structural preconditions"
**Conclusion**：同步修改 "resolves" 相关措辞

#### W3 [MF4+SF7, CRITICAL]: HCAPO 等 5 篇引用 + Related Work positioning

在 references.bib 添加：
```bibtex
@article{hcapo2026,
  title   = {Hindsight Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon {LLM} Agents},
  author  = {Tan, ...},
  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2603.08754},
  year    = {2026},
}

@article{rcgrpo2026,
  title   = {{RC-GRPO}: ...},
  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2602.03025},
  year    = {2026},
}

@article{istar2025,
  title   = {{iStar}: Implicit Step-Level Reward for Multi-Turn Agent Tasks},
  author  = {Liu, ...},
  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2509.19199},
  year    = {2025},
}

@article{agenticrl_survey2026,
  title   = {From Reasoning to Agentic: A Survey on Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning for {LLMs}},
  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2604.09459},
  year    = {2026},
}

@article{edgegrpo2025,
  title   = {{EDGE-GRPO}: ...},
  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2507.21848},
  year    = {2025},
}
```
**必须 WebSearch 查找每篇论文的完整 title 和 author list 再写入 bib。**

在 related_work.tex "Multi-turn credit assignment methods" 段落末尾添加 HCAPO positioning：
"HCAPO~\cite{hcapo2026} applies hindsight credit assignment to ALFWorld agents, achieving strong performance with step-level credit. This validates our diagnosis: sign correctness---not credit granularity per se---is the critical bottleneck. When step-level methods employ proper credit assignment (as HCAPO does through hindsight), they can succeed; the failure lies in the \textit{default} step-independent windowing that assigns credit based on episode outcome alone."

在 introduction.tex P2 修改：
"Concurrent methods that improve per-step credit quality (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}, GAGPO~\cite{gagpo2026}, Turn-PPO~\cite{turnppo2026})" → 添加 HCAPO，并将 "address symptoms but not the structural root cause: the windowing itself" 修改为更精确表述：
"address the symptom through improved credit assignment mechanisms, validating that sign correctness is the critical factor. Our contribution is orthogonal: we formally diagnose \textit{why} the default windowing fails."

#### W4 [MF3]: Cell C 缺失论证加强
在 discussion.tex 添加（在 "Generality and mechanism interaction" 段后或合适位置）：
"The A→B comparison provides direct evidence for M2: both conditions use identical observation scope ($h{=}2$), differing only in advantage granularity (step-level vs.\ episode-level). Eliminating step-level advantage converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning. Cell~C would provide independent confirmation of M2 in isolation; its consistent training instability (Appendix~\ref{app:cell_c}) prevented this validation."

#### W5 [SF1]: Definition 1 操作化循环
在 experiments.tex Section 4.4 (sign verification) 添加：
"This operationalization is a conservative approximation: successful episodes may contain suboptimal windows, making our label an upper bound on true local correctness. Even under this generous criterion, credit agreement is indistinguishable from random chance, strengthening the diagnosis."

#### W6 [SF2]: cliff → threshold effect
在 experiments.tex，将 "A credit assignment cliff between" 改为 "A credit assignment threshold between"。
将 "This cliff pattern" 改为 "This threshold pattern"。

#### W7 [SF6]: SFT 训练细节
在 appendix.tex Hyperparameters section 之后添加 SFT baseline 训练细节：
- 2,546 demonstrations 的来源（从 RAGEN 框架文档或代码中查找）
- SFT 训练超参数（learning rate, epochs, batch size 等）
如果代码中找不到细节，写 "We use the expert demonstrations provided by the ALFWorld benchmark following the protocol of RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025}."

#### W8 [m1-m5]: Minor 修复
- m1: 统一术语为 "step-independent" 作为主术语，其他用法改为 "step-independent windowing"
- m2: 将 Proposition 2 proof 中 "locally good actions" 改为 "locally correct actions"（与 Definition 1 一致）
- m3: Section 3 "effective learning rate 12.6%" → "12.5%"
- m4: ScienceWorld Section 4.7 添加说明 30.1% 和 34.8% 是不同 eval 配置（如 temperature 不同）
- m5: 色盲配色暂不改（需重新绘图，优先级低）

---

## 批次 2：实验结果整合（实验完成后）

实验 A 结果出来后：
1. 在 Section 4.4 添加 binary reward credit agreement 数据："Under pure binary reward (no penalty), credit agreement drops to X\% (n=..., 95\% CI [..., ...]), closely matching the theoretical prediction of 19.1\%. The gap between X\% (binary) and 52\% (penalty) confirms that penalty-induced within-group variance partially decorrelates advantage sign from episode outcome."
2. 更新 abstract/intro 的数字叙述：以 52%（实测主数字）和 X%（binary 验证）双线呈现
3. 将 Appendix A.2 的 penalty reward 分析核心结论提升到主文

实验 B 结果：将 binomial test p-value + CI 加入 Section 4.4 "52.07%" 句子后。

实验 C 结果（如有）：在 Eq.6 修改段落中引用实际 |A_correct|/|A_wrong| 比值。

---

修完后编译确认 ≤8 页，把所有 CRITICAL 修改（Eq.6 重写、Title 改、HCAPO positioning）+ 实验结果发给我。

1	\section{Theoretical Analysis}
2	\label{sec:theory}
3	
4	Sign misattribution is the more fundamental failure mechanism because it causes active divergence, not merely slow convergence. We derive the probability of incorrect gradient direction below; full proofs appear in Appendix~\ref{app:theory_full}.
5	
6	\textbf{Definition 1} (Locally correct action). \textit{An action $a_t$ is \textbf{locally correct} if it would receive positive advantage under an oracle that observes the full episode history $h_t$ and has access to the true action-value function. Equivalently, window $k$ contains locally correct actions if replacing them (holding all other windows fixed) would decrease the probability of episode success.}
7	
8	\textbf{Proposition 1.} \textit{Under step-independent training with binary episode reward $R_{\text{episode}} \in \{0, r_+\}$, the probability that window $k$'s advantage $\hat{A}_k$ has the correct sign (aligned with local action quality) approaches the base success rate $p$ as the number of rollouts $K$ per group increases.}
9	
10	\textit{Proof sketch.} In a failed episode ($R_{\text{episode}} = 0$), all windows receive $R_k = 0$. If any rollout in the group succeeded, $\mu > 0$, so every window in the failed episode receives $\hat{A}_k < 0$---including windows with locally correct actions. The probability of receiving the wrong sign in a failed episode is $1 - (1-p)^{K-1}$ (at least one group member succeeded). As $K$ grows, this approaches $1-p$, and the overall probability of correct sign approaches $p$. \hfill$\square$
11	
12	The key distinction is temporal. Information loss (M1) imposes a constant ceiling determined by $h$; sign misattribution (M2) is cumulative---each wrong-sign gradient step erodes policy competence below that ceiling. A wrong-sign gradient moves the optimizer \textit{away} from good policies; a reduced-magnitude gradient from discounting ($\gamma^{(N-k)}$) still points toward good policies. Sign errors produce active diverge
…[truncated 4041 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{RL for multi-turn LLM agents.}
5	Policy gradient methods---PPO~\cite{schulman2017ppo}, GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, DPO~\cite{rafailov2023dpo}---underpin RLHF~\cite{ouyang2022instructgpt} and have been extended to multi-turn agents~\cite{yao2023react} through step-independent windowing~\cite{ragen2025}. Subsequent work improves algorithms (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}), reward design (Self-Guide~\cite{selfguide2026}), or training pipelines (SPEAR~\cite{spear2025}, DigiRL~\cite{digiRL2024}), but all share a common assumption: trajectory decomposition into fixed windows is benign. We show this assumption is false---the windowing introduces a structural credit assignment pathology that no algorithmic improvement within the paradigm can fix.
6	
7	\paragraph{Credit assignment in reinforcement learning.}
8	TD($\lambda$)~\cite{sutton1988td}, GAE~\cite{schulman2016gae}, and TRPO~\cite{schulman2015trpo} interpolate between bootstrapping and Monte Carlo returns; REINFORCE~\cite{williams1992reinforce} uses full episode returns. Reward shaping~\cite{ng1999shaping} and hindsight experience replay~\cite{andrychowicz2017her} address sparse reward in classical RL; counterfactual baselines~\cite{foerster2018coma} handle team reward credit assignment. Process reward models~\cite{lightman2024lets,wang2024mathshepherd} provide step-level supervision for reasoning tasks. In the low-success-rate regime ($p < 0.2$) typical of agent tasks, the classical bias-variance tradeoff breaks down: Proposition~2 shows sign errors under windowing grow as $1{-}p$, dominating any variance reduction that windowing provides.
9	
10	\paragraph{Credit assignment and sign misattribution evidence.}
11	Several independent studies corroborate our diagnosis. IRC~\cite{irc2026} reports per-turn advantages frequently disagree in sign with outcome advantages. MC-GRPO~\cite{mcgrpo2025} measures a 5\% sign-flip rate. GRPO-$\lambda$~\cite{grpolambd
…[truncated 1431 chars]

  "new_string": "where $P_c = P(\\text{correct} \\mid \\text{update})$, $g_+$ points toward $\\theta^*$, and $g_-$ points away. However, at $p{=}12.5\\%$, $K{=}8$, 80.9\\% of individual updates carry incorrect sign, producing extreme gradient variance. In this variance-dominated regime, the weak positive expectation provides little practical benefit: individual optimization steps overwhelmingly move away from $\\theta^*$, preventing reliable convergence. The M1--M2 positive feedback loop amplifies this: information loss (M1) caps the success rate $p$; lower $p$ increases wrong-sign frequency under M2 (Equation~\\ref{eq:correct_conditional}), which further depresses $p$, driving the empirically observed collapse."
}

_source line 3690 · 2026-05-23T09:33:07.305336+00:00_

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Generality and mechanism interaction.}
5	Sign misattribution arises whenever three structural properties co-occur: (a) step-level windowing, (b) sparse binary reward, and (c) group-relative normalization. Under these conditions, the advantage sign collapses to a proxy for episode outcome regardless of local quality, and severity scales with task difficulty ($P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) \to p$). M1 and M2 interact in a vicious cycle: M1 lowers $p$ by preventing multi-step strategies, increasing wrong-sign updates under M2, which further degrades $p$---explaining why step-independent training produces active collapse rather than slow convergence.
6	
7	\paragraph{Why step-independent became the default.}
8	Six contributing factors, from context length constraints to framework design inertia, are detailed in Appendix~\ref{app:why_defaults}.
9	
10	\paragraph{Seed-dependent OOD generalization.}
11	The multi-seed evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:seed_variability}) reveals a bimodal distribution: a stagnant cluster (10--16\% OOD, NS vs.\ SFT) and a generalized cluster (25--31\%, $p{<}0.001$). Training duration does not explain the gap (seed100: $+$1.50pp from step~5 to~10; seed5: 25.37\% at step~10), pointing to early training dynamics or initialization sensitivity. Identifying this mechanism is an important open question.
12	
13	\paragraph{Practitioner guidance.}
14	Use episode-level credit when the full episode fits in context; otherwise use the largest feasible $h$ ($h{\geq}20$; $h{\leq}10$ collapses). Finer-grained methods (Turn-PPO, GAGPO) should be applied only after establishing an episode-level baseline, as they assume correct gradient direction.
15	
16

55	
56	\paragraph{Window-size sweep.} A credit assignment cliff between $h{=}10$ and $h{=}20$ separates collapse from recovery (Figure~\ref{fig:hsweep}b; seed~1): $h{=}2$: 0\%, $h{=}5$: 0\%, $h{=}10$: 6.25\%, $h{=}20$: 15.6\%, episode-level: 23.88\%. This cliff pattern is observed in a single seed; multi-seed verification is needed to confirm its robustness. Only episode-level training achieves substantial generalization.% exp_id: h_sweep_series, exp029_seed1
57	\label{sec:results_hsweep}
58	
59	\subsection{Sign Misattribution Verification}
60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), indistinguishable from 50\% random chance. Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63	
64	\subsection{Episode-Level Training: Seed Variability}
65	\label{sec:seed_variability}
66	
67	Episode-level GRPO resolves the dual-mechanism failure, but training outcomes remain seed-dependent. We systematically evaluate $N{=}11$ random seeds under identical hyperparameters, revealing a $2{\times}2$ structure: \textit{training stability} (collapsed vs.\ non-collapsed) $\times$ \textit{OOD generalization} (stagnant vs.\ generalized).
68	
69	We define \textbf{collapsed} seeds as those where in-distribution success declines $>$50\% from peak and remains below baseline for $\geq$3 consecutive steps. \textbf{Non-collapsed} seeds are the complement. Among non-collapsed seeds, we further distinguish \textbf{generalized} (Fisher exa

  "old_string": "These methods improve per-step signal quality. Our work is complementary: we diagnose \\textit{why} step-independent training fails, informing when finer-grained credit is safe to use (only after establishing correct gradient direction via episode-level baselines).",
  "new_string": "These methods improve per-step signal quality. HCAPO~\\cite{hcapo2026} applies hindsight credit assignment to ALFWorld agents, achieving strong performance with step-level credit. This validates our diagnosis: sign correctness, not credit granularity per se, is the critical bottleneck. When step-level methods employ proper credit assignment (as HCAPO does through hindsight), they can succeed; the failure lies in the default step-independent windowing that assigns credit based on episode outcome alone. Our work is complementary: we diagnose \\textit{why} step-independent training fails, informing when finer-grained credit is safe to use (only after establishing correct gradient direction via episode-level baselines)."
}
```

</details>

This validates our diagnosis: sign correctness, not credit granularity per se, is the critical bottleneck. _source line 3726 · 2026-05-23T09:34:14.500508+00:00_

84	\end{table}
85	
86	All 1.7B experiments were conducted on a single NVIDIA A100 80\,GB GPU. Each training run (15 steps, $K{=}8$ rollouts per task, 16 tasks per step) required approximately 6--8 GPU-hours. The 11-seed evaluation totaled ${\sim}$80 GPU-hours. The 8B experiment used 4$\times$ A100 80\,GB with FSDP, requiring ${\sim}$24 GPU-hours per run. ScienceWorld experiments used the same single-GPU setup as the 1.7B ALFWorld runs.
87	
88	\section{Additional Conditions}
89	\label{app:additional}
90	
91	All conditions use single seeds unless noted.
92	
93	\paragraph{Dense reward.} Step-independent $h{=}2$ with $+0.15$ per valid action. Action validity rises from 60\% to 97.5\% while task success remains 0\%---the agent games format-level rewards without solving tasks (Figure~\ref{fig:additional_conditions}b).

  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：纯 Binary Reward 下的 Credit Agreement 测量\n\n### 目标\n在纯 binary reward（无 invalid action penalty）下运行 Cell A (h=2, step-independent) 训练 2-3 步，从 rollout 数据提取 advantage sign，计算 credit agreement rate。\n\n理论预测（Proposition 2）：在 p=12.5%, K=8 下，P(correct sign | update) = 19.1%。当前论文中 penalty reward 下测到 52.07%。本实验验证去除 penalty 后是否接近理论值 19.1%。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：bjb1-17606（root@bjb1-17606）\n- GPU：cuda:0\n- 项目路径：/root/cost-verify-agent-rl\n- 环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. **SSH 连接并探测环境**：\n   - 确认 RAGEN 代码目录存在：`/root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN`\n   - 查看现有 Cell A 配置文件，了解配置结构\n   - 查看 cuda:0 是否空闲（nvidia-smi）\n\n2. **创建配置**：\n   - 基于现有 Cell A (h=2) 配置，创建新配置文件\n   - 关键修改：`invalid_action_penalty = 0`（纯 binary reward，R ∈ {0, 1}）\n   - 保持其他参数不变：h=2, K=8, step-independent windowing\n   - total_steps = 3（只需要足够数据，不需要训到收敛）\n   - save_freq = 5（不需要保存 checkpoint）\n\n3. **查找 advantage sign 提取方法**：\n   - 检查 RAGEN 代码中如何记录 advantage 值\n   - 寻找训练日志中是否有 per-window advantage 数据\n   - 如果没有现成日志，需要在训练代码中添加 advantage sign 记录\n\n4. **运行训练**：\n   - 用 submit_training_job 提交，不要用 nohup\n   - 命令格式参考：\n     ```bash\n     source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 RAY_ADDRESS='' WANDB_DIR=/root/autodl-tmp/wandb_tmp python train.py --config-name &lt;your_config>\n     ```\n\n5. **分析**：训练完成后，提取 advantage sign 数据，计算：\n   - Credit agreement rate = P(advantage > 0 | window in successful episode with valid actions)\n   - 样本量 n\n   - Wilson 95% CI\n   - Binomial test p-value (H0: P=0.5, H0: P=0.191)\n\n### 重要注意\n- 只用 cuda:0，不要用其他 GPU\n- 如果 cuda:0 被其他进程占用，报告回来，不要强制启动\n- 检查现有 Cell A 实验（如 7b_cell_a_h2）的代码和配置作为参考\n- 如果发现 RAGEN 没有记录 per-window advantage，需要修改代码添加记录——但先检查是否已有现成数据\n\n### 产出\n将结果写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_credit_agreement_results.md`，包含：\n- Credit agreement rate (%)\n- 样本量\n- 95% CI\n- Binomial test results\n- 数据来源和计算方法说明",

{
  "task_type": "analysis:binomial_test_advantage_ratio",
  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：从现有实验数据计算 Binomial Test + Advantage 量级分析\n\n### 实验 B：52.07% Credit Agreement 的统计检验\n\n论文 Section 4.4 报告 step-independent (h=2) 下 credit agreement rate = 52.07%。需要：\n\n1. **找到原始数据**：\n   - SSH 到 bjb1-17606（root@bjb1-17606，环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`）\n   - 项目路径：/root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN\n   - 查找 exp_id = 7b_cell_a_h2 相关的训练日志/分析脚本\n   - 搜索关键词：credit_agreement, advantage_sign, sign_verification\n   - 可能的路径：/root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/analysis/ 或 /root/autodl-tmp/logs/\n\n2. **提取样本量**：确定 52.07% 是基于多少个 window 样本计算的（n=?）\n\n3. **计算统计检验**：\n   - **Binomial test 1**: H0: P=0.5 (random chance), 计算 p-value\n   - **Binomial test 2**: H0: P=0.191 (binary theory prediction), 计算 p-value\n   - **Wilson 95% CI** for the 52.07% estimate\n\n### 实验 C：Advantage 量级分布\n\n**目的**：验证 GRPO normalization 下 |A_correct| / |A_wrong| ≈ K-1 = 7\n\n1. **从同一训练日志提取 advantage 值**（如果日志中有 per-window advantage 数据）\n2. **分组**：\n   - Correct sign windows: 成功 episode 中的 windows（advantage 应为正）\n   - Incorrect sign windows: 失败 episode 中的 windows（advantage 为负但 locally correct）\n3. **计算**：\n   - Mean |A_correct| 和 Mean |A_wrong|\n   - 比值 |A_correct| / |A_wrong|\n   - 理论预测：≈ K-1 = 7\n\n### 如果找不到 advantage 数据\n\n如果训练日志中没有 per-window advantage 值：\n- 检查 W&B（项目可能是 cost-verify-agent-rl 或 RAGEN 相关）\n- 检查 RAGEN 代码中 advantage 计算的位置，确认是否有现成的 logging\n- 如果确实没有记录，报告\"advantage 量级数据不可用\"，只完成 Binomial test 部分（基于 52.07% 和合理的 n 估计）\n\n### 产出\n写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binomial_test_results.md`：\n- 样本量 n\n- Binomial test p-values (两个 H0)\n- Wilson 95% CI\n- Advantage magnitude ratio（如有数据）\n- 数据来源说明",
  "tool_groups": "['ssh', 'registry', 'knowledge']"
}

[Director 指令] [Director 紧急修正] Eq.6 和 52% 措辞——覆盖先前指令

Reviewer 修正了先前的分析，以下覆盖先前 W1 和实验 B 的指令：

## 修正 1: Eq.6 — 不要大幅删除，改为微调

**先前指令错误**：先前让你删除 "monotonic divergence" 和 "comparable magnitude" 假设。Reviewer 重新分析后发现 per-group 加权 |A_correct|/|A_wrong| 实际是 **1.32**（不是 7），"comparable magnitude" 假设近似正确。

**正确修改**：
- **保留** Eq.6 的基本结构
- **保留** "comparable magnitude" 但改为有数据支撑："Empirical measurement from training logs confirms approximately comparable per-group advantage magnitudes ($|\hat{A}_{\text{correct}}|/|\hat{A}_{\text{wrong}}| \approx 1.32$)."
- 将 "monotonic divergence" 或任何 "strict divergence" 措辞弱化为："The expected gradient thus points weakly away from good policies. Combined with the M1--M2 positive feedback loop (Section~\ref{sec:dual_mechanism}), this weak directional bias is amplified into the empirically observed collapse."
- 如果有 "After t steps, ||θ_t - θ*|| ≥ ..." 的 linear bound，改为 "The cumulative effect of this directional bias, amplified by the M1--M2 feedback loop, drives progressive policy degradation."
- 补充 88% advantage 分布重叠和 point-biserial r=0.068 作为支撑证据（如果这些数据来自 worker 分析）

## 修正 2: "indistinguishable from 50%" 措辞 — 必须修改

52.07% vs 50% 在 n≈3000 下统计显著（p=0.025, Wilson CI [50.3%, 53.9%]）。

**4 处需修改**（abstract, experiments §4.4, conclusion, appendix）：
- 将所有 "indistinguishable from 50% random chance" 或类似表述
- 改为 "marginally above random chance (52.07\%, $p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2.07pp effect size)"
- 或更简洁版："near-random (52.07\%, $+$2.07pp above chance)"

核心信息不变——2pp 实践上无意义，但 "indistinguishable" 技术不准确。

**其他所有指令不变**（Title 改 Correction、HCAPO positioning、Cell C 论证、Definition 1 等）。

_source line 3756 · 2026-05-23T10:06:59.186827+00:00_

34	
35	\paragraph{Cumulative divergence.} Sign misattribution produces a systematic gradient bias. Let $\theta^*$ denote an optimal policy parameterization. At each update, the expected gradient under step-independent training is:
36	\begin{equation}
37	\mathbb{E}[\nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}] = P_c \cdot g_+ + (1 - P_c) \cdot g_-
38	\label{eq:biased_gradient}
39	\end{equation}
40	where $P_c = P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update})$, $g_+$ points toward $\theta^*$, and $g_-$ points away. GRPO normalization creates a magnitude asymmetry: in a group with one success and $K{-}1$ failures, the successful rollout's normalized advantage scales as ${\sim}(K{-}1)$ times larger than each failed rollout's, making the expected gradient weakly positive even when $P_c < 0.5$. However, at $p{=}12.5\%$, $K{=}8$, 80.9\% of individual updates carry incorrect sign, producing extreme gradient variance. In this variance-dominated regime, the weak positive expectation provides little practical benefit: individual optimization steps overwhelmingly move away from $\theta^*$, preventing reliable convergence. The M1--M2 positive feedback loop amplifies this: information loss (M1) caps the success rate $p$; lower $p$ increases wrong-sign frequency under M2 (Equation~\ref{eq:correct_conditional}), which further depresses $p$, driving the empirically observed collapse.
41

_source line 3762 · 2026-05-23T10:07:19.673882+00:00_

60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), indistinguishable from 50\% random chance. Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver. This operationalization is a conservative approximation: successful episodes may contain suboptimal windows, making our label an upper bound on true local correctness. Even under this generous criterion, credit agreement is indistinguishable from random chance, strengthening the diagnosis.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63

100	
101	Three distinct outcomes emerge. 3/11 seeds collapse (27\%), with response length inflating to 14{,}000--16{,}000 tokens (Appendix~\ref{app:response_length}); 4/11 are non-collapsed but stagnant (36\%), with OOD indistinguishable from SFT (10.45--15.67\%, all NS at $\alpha{=}0.01$); and 4/11 generalize (36\%), reaching 23.88--30.60\% OOD ($p{<}0.001$). No collapsed seed generalizes, confirming stable training is necessary. One seed (seed2) shows delayed learning: 15.67\% OOD after 15 steps ($p{=}0.02$, borderline).% exp_id: exp029 multi-seed series
102	
103	The stagnant--generalized gap is not explained by insufficient training: seed100 improves only 1.50pp from step~5 to step~10 (11.19\% $\to$ 12.69\%, both NS), while seed5 reaches 25.37\% at step~10. Stable training is necessary but not sufficient for OOD generalization---the mechanism driving seed-dependent transfer remains an open question (Section~\ref{sec:discussion}).

_source line 3766 · 2026-05-23T10:07:21.031071+00:00_

24	\begin{abstract}
25	Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning requires credit assignment across long interaction trajectories, yet current frameworks default to step-independent windowing that decomposes trajectories into independently optimized windows. This introduces a structural credit assignment pathology driven by two mechanisms: \textit{policy information loss}, which truncates context and imposes a constant performance ceiling, and \textit{advantage sign misattribution}, which causes gradient updates to reflect episode outcomes rather than local action quality, producing cumulative degradation. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with group size $K{=}8$, theory predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign under binary reward; empirical measurement under penalty reward yields a 52\% credit agreement rate, indistinguishable from random chance. A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms: eliminating sign misattribution converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning; removing information loss enables generalization. Episode-level Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), treating the full trajectory as a single optimization sample, addresses both structural preconditions. A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% of seeds avoid collapse, but only 36\% achieve statistically significant out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization ($p{<}0.01$; 24--31\% OOD vs.\ 6.7\% supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline). Preliminary single-seed cross-scale verification at 8B shows both failure and fix (90.3\% OOD vs.\ collapse to 1.5\%), and cross-environment validation on ScienceWorld provides evidence the collapse extends beyond ALFWorld.
26	\end{abstract}

_source line 3768 · 2026-05-23T10:07:21.634117+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Step-independent training in multi-turn agent RL fails through two mechanisms: policy information loss imposes a performance ceiling, while advantage sign misattribution causes cumulative degradation: at $p{=}12.5\%$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}$80.9\% incorrect-sign updates under binary reward (52\% measured under penalty reward, consistent with near-random credit assignment). A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms; episode-level GRPO addresses both (17.2\% mean OOD at 1.7B; preliminary single-seed 8B evidence shows 90.3\% OOD). A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% avoid collapse, but only 36\% generalize ($p{<}0.01$). The central insight---sign errors are more destructive than magnitude imprecision---applies to any agent RL method combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization.
5

28	\textit{Remark.} Proposition~2 assumes binary episode rewards for analytical tractability. Under non-binary rewards (e.g., with per-step penalties for invalid actions), the group mean $\mu$ is nonzero even when all episodes fail, but the qualitative conclusion is preserved: advantage sign is determined by relative episode performance within the group, not by local action quality within individual windows. Section~\ref{sec:sign_verification} validates this empirically using the full reward structure.
29	
30	\textit{Penalty reward effect on credit agreement.} Proposition~2 predicts 19.1\% correct-sign rate (i.e., 80.9\% incorrect) under pure binary reward. Our experiments include a $-0.1$ penalty per invalid action, making the reward non-binary. This penalty introduces variance within failed episodes: windows with valid actions receive $R_k = 0$ while windows with invalid actions receive $R_k = -0.1h$. Within a failed episode, the penalty-induced variance can occasionally produce positive $\hat{A}_k$ for windows with valid actions even when $\mu > 0$, since the normalization accounts for intra-group variance. This shifts the empirical credit agreement from the theoretical 19.1\% toward 52\%---still indistinguishable from the 50\% random baseline and far below the level needed for convergence. The gap between 19.1\% (binary theory) and 52\% (penalty-reward measurement) thus reflects the reward structure difference, not a failure of the theoretical framework.
31

Of the 65.6\% of cases that produce a gradient update, only $P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) = 0.125 / 0.656 \approx 19.1\%$ have the correct sign. When $P_c < 0.5$ (which holds for $p < 0.5$; Equation~\\ref{eq:correct_conditional}), the expected gradient points weakly away from good policies. The cumulative effect of this directional bias, amplified by the M1--M2 positive feedback loop (Section~\\ref{sec:dual_mechanism}), drives progressive policy degradation: information loss (M1) caps $p$; lower $p$ increases wrong-sign frequency under M2, which further depresses $p$, producing the empirically observed collapse."

3	**Source**: hsweep_h2_seed2 (W&B: jhwyg4tc), Cell A (h=2, step-independent GRPO)

7	## Experiment B: Binomial Test on 52.07% Credit Agreement
8	
9	### Data
10

12	|-----------|-------|

29	**Interpretation**: The 52.07% credit agreement is statistically distinguishable from 50% random chance (p = 0.025). However, the effect size is trivially small (+2.07 percentage points), meaning the advantage sign carries almost no practical information about episode outcome. The current paper text says "indistinguishable from 50% random chance" — this should be revised to "marginally above 50% (p = 0.025) but with negligible practical significance" or "near-random."

_source line 3794 · 2026-05-23T10:09:41.761836+00:00_

59	\subsection{Sign Misattribution Verification}
60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), marginally above random chance ($p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2.07pp effect size). Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver. This operationalization is a conservative approximation: successful episodes may contain suboptimal windows, making our label an upper bound on true local correctness. Even under this generous criterion, credit agreement remains near-random, strengthening the diagnosis.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63	
64	\subsection{Episode-Level Training: Seed Variability}

28	\textit{Remark.} Proposition~2 assumes binary episode rewards for analytical tractability. Under non-binary rewards (e.g., with per-step penalties for invalid actions), the group mean $\mu$ is nonzero even when all episodes fail, but the qualitative conclusion is preserved: advantage sign is determined by relative episode performance within the group, not by local action quality within individual windows. Section~\ref{sec:sign_verification} validates this empirically using the full reward structure.
29	
30	\textit{Penalty reward effect on credit agreement.} Proposition~2 predicts 19.1\% correct-sign rate (i.e., 80.9\% incorrect) under pure binary reward. Our experiments include a $-0.1$ penalty per invalid action, making the reward non-binary. This penalty introduces variance within failed episodes: windows with valid actions receive $R_k = 0$ while windows with invalid actions receive $R_k = -0.1h$. Within a failed episode, the penalty-induced variance can occasionally produce positive $\hat{A}_k$ for windows with valid actions even when $\mu > 0$, since the normalization accounts for intra-group variance. This shifts the empirical credit agreement from the theoretical 19.1\% toward 52\%---still marginally above the 50\% random baseline ($p{=}0.025$; $+$2pp effect size) and far below the level needed for convergence. The gap between 19.1\% (binary theory) and 52\% (penalty-reward measurement) thus reflects the reward structure difference, not a failure of the theoretical framework.
31	
32	We evaluate these expressions at the empirical baseline of our experiments. At $p = 12.5\%$ and $K = 8$: $(1-p)^K = 0.875^8 \approx 0.344$, giving $P(\text{correct or no update}) \approx 0.469$. Of the 65.6\% of cases that produce a gradient update, only $P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) = 0.125 / 0.656 \approx 19.1\%$ have the correct sign. As $K$ increases to 16, $(1-p)^{K} \approx 0.118$, and $P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) \approx 14.2\%$. In the large-$K$ limit,

`ps -p 14928 -o pid,stat,etime,cmd` 看进程是否还在\n   - `tail -30 /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_training.log` 看训练进度\n   - 如果训练已完成或失败，查看日志中的最终状态\n\n2. **检查 advantage 日志**：\n   - `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl` 看文件是否存在\n   - 如果存在，`wc -l` 和 `head -5` 查看格式和数据量\n\n3. **如果训练仍在运行**：\n   - 报告当前进度（在第几个 step）\n   - 预估剩余时间\n   - 结束 Worker 并说明\"训练仍在进行，需要再次检查\"\n\n4. **如果训练已完成**：\n   分析 advantage 日志，计算 credit agreement rate：\n\n   **方法**：\n   - 对每个 episode：如果 episode reward > 0（成功），则该 episode 所有 valid action windows 标记为 \"locally correct\"\n   - Credit agreement = P(advantage > 0 | locally correct window)\n   - 由于是纯 binary reward (penalty=0)，理论预测值 = 19.1% (Proposition 2, p=12.5%, K=8)\n\n   **如果 RAGEN 没有记录 per-window advantage**：\n   - 检查 W&B 日志是否有 advantage 数据\n   - 检查 RAGEN 代码中 LOG_PER_SAMPLE_ADV 环境变量是否被支持\n   - 如果数据不可用，从 rollout 数据重建：提取 episode rewards，计算 GRPO advantages\n\n   **计算**：\n   ```python\n   # Credit agreement rate\n   n_correct_sign = sum(1 for w in locally_correct_windows if w.advantage > 0)\n   n_total = len(locally_correct_windows)\n   rate = n_correct_sign / n_total\n   \n   # Wilson 95% CI\n   from scipy.stats import binom\n   # or manual: (p + z²/2n ± z√(p(1-p)/n + z²/4n²)) / (1 + z²/n)\n   \n   # Binomial tests\n   # H0: P = 0.5 (random chance)\n   # H0: P = 0.191 (binary theory prediction)\n   ```\n\n5. **写入结果**：\n   `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_credit_agreement_results.md`\n\n   包含：\n   - Credit agreement rate (%)\n   - 样本量 n\n   - Wilson 95% CI\n   - Binomial test vs H0=0.5: p-value\n   - Binomial test vs H0=0.191: p-value\n   - 与理论预测 19.1% 的对比\n   - 数据来源说明\n\n### 服务器\n- bjb1-17606, account: root\n- 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`",
  "tool_groups": "['ssh', 'knowledge']"
}

修复：在 related_work.tex 适当位置添加 cite：
- "GRPO collapse phenomena" 段落：添加 RC-GRPO~\cite{rcgrpo2026} 和 EDGE-GRPO~\cite{edgegrpo2025}（都是 GRPO advantage collapse / reward miscalibration 相关）
- "Credit assignment in reinforcement learning" 段落或 "RL for multi-turn LLM agents" 段落：添加 iStar~\cite{istar2025}（implicit step rewards）
- "RL for multi-turn LLM agents" 段落末尾或 "Multi-turn credit assignment methods" 段落：添加 survey~\cite{agenticrl_survey2026}

每篇 1-2 词描述即可，不需要长句。例如："RC-GRPO~\cite{rcgrpo2026} addresses reward-conditioned variance collapse, and EDGE-GRPO~\cite{edgegrpo2025} proposes entropy-driven advantage diversity."

### P2: related_work.tex 第 1 段仍有过强声明
第 5 行 "no algorithmic improvement within the paradigm can fix" 与 intro P2 的修改不一致（intro 已改为 "address the symptom through improved credit assignment mechanisms"）。

修复：将 "no algorithmic improvement within the paradigm can fix" 改为 "the windowing introduces a structural credit assignment pathology that persists under default configurations"

### P3: intro P3 一致性
第 8 行 "sign errors cause active divergence, not merely slow convergence" 与 Eq.6 修改（"expected gradient points weakly away... amplified by feedback loop"）有张力。

修复：改为 "sign errors, amplified by the M1--M2 feedback loop, cause progressive degradation rather than merely slow convergence"

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{RL for multi-turn LLM agents.}
5	Policy gradient methods---PPO~\cite{schulman2017ppo}, GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, DPO~\cite{rafailov2023dpo}---underpin RLHF~\cite{ouyang2022instructgpt} and have been extended to multi-turn agents~\cite{yao2023react} through step-independent windowing~\cite{ragen2025}. Subsequent work improves algorithms (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}), reward design (Self-Guide~\cite{selfguide2026}), or training pipelines (SPEAR~\cite{spear2025}, DigiRL~\cite{digiRL2024}), but all share a common assumption: trajectory decomposition into fixed windows is benign. We show this assumption is false---the windowing introduces a structural credit assignment pathology that no algorithmic improvement within the paradigm can fix.
6	
7	\paragraph{Credit assignment in reinforcement learning.}
8	TD($\lambda$)~\cite{sutton1988td}, GAE~\cite{schulman2016gae}, and TRPO~\cite{schulman2015trpo} interpolate between bootstrapping and Monte Carlo returns; REINFORCE~\cite{williams1992reinforce} uses full episode returns. Reward shaping~\cite{ng1999shaping} and hindsight experience replay~\cite{andrychowicz2017her} address sparse reward in classical RL; counterfactual baselines~\cite{foerster2018coma} handle team reward credit assignment. Process reward models~\cite{lightman2024lets,wang2024mathshepherd} provide step-level supervision for reasoning tasks. In the low-success-rate regime ($p < 0.2$) typical of agent tasks, the classical bias-variance tradeoff breaks down: Proposition~2 shows sign errors under windowing grow as $1{-}p$, dominating any variance reduction that windowing provides.
9	
10	\paragraph{Credit assignment and sign misattribution evidence.}
11	Several independent studies corroborate our diagnosis. IRC~\cite{irc2026} reports per-turn advantages frequently disagree in sign with outcome advantages. MC-GRPO~\cite{mcgrpo2025} measures a 5\% sign-flip rate. GRPO-$\lambda$~\cite{grpolambd
…[truncated 1890 chars]

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Reinforcement learning from sparse terminal reward has emerged as a promising paradigm for training LLM agents on multi-turn interactive tasks spanning 30--50 interaction steps~\cite{kim2025reflact,digiRL2024,wang2022scienceworld}. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, with its critic-free design, has become the algorithm of choice. Yet a gap persists between the promise of RL-driven agent improvement and the reality of training outcomes: recent agent RL systems~\cite{ragen2025,selfguide2026,spear2025,feng2025gigpo} share a common, unscrutinized implementation pattern---decomposing trajectories into fixed-size windows, each optimized independently---and frequently report training instability without diagnosing its root cause.
5	
6	We show this \textit{step-independent} default introduces a fundamental credit assignment pathology. Step-independent training partitions trajectories into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each optimized via GRPO. This produces a two-phase collapse: task success drops to 0\% while action validity initially remains high, then continued training destroys even basic formatting. Standard remedies---dense reward shaping, discount tuning---fail. Concurrent methods that improve per-step credit quality (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}, GAGPO~\cite{gagpo2026}, Turn-PPO~\cite{turnppo2026}, HCAPO~\cite{hcapo2026}) address the symptom through improved credit assignment mechanisms, validating that sign correctness is the critical factor. Our contribution is orthogonal: we formally diagnose \textit{why} the default windowing fails.
7	
8	We trace the collapse to two mechanisms at different layers. \textit{\textbf{Policy information loss}} (M1) operates at the inference layer: each window lacks access to earlier observations, imposing a constant performance ceiling. \textit{\textbf{Advantage sign misattribution}} (M2) operates at the training layer: under binary reward, 
…[truncated 2036 chars]

读取数据\n      data = []\n      with open('/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl') as f:\n          for line in f:\n              data.append(json.loads(line))\n      \n      # 计算 credit agreement\n      # locally correct = window in successful episode with valid actions\n      # credit agreement = P(advantage > 0 | locally correct)\n      correct_sign = 0\n      total_correct_windows = 0\n      for entry in data:\n          if entry.get('episode_success', False):\n              for window in entry.get('windows', []):\n                  if window.get('valid_action', True):\n                      total_correct_windows += 1\n                      if window.get('advantage', 0) > 0:\n                          correct_sign += 1\n      \n      rate = correct_sign / total_correct_windows if total_correct_windows > 0 else 0\n      ```\n      \n      注意：日志格式可能不同，先用 head 查看实际格式再写分析代码。\n\n   c. 如果 advantage 日志不存在，检查 RAGEN 代码中 LOG_PER_SAMPLE_ADV 是否被支持：\n      ```\n      grep -r \"LOG_PER_SAMPLE_ADV\" /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/\n      ```\n      如果不支持，尝试从 W&B 日志或训练输出中提取 advantage 数据。\n\n   d. 计算统计量：\n      - Credit agreement rate (%)\n      - 样本量 n\n      - Wilson 95% CI\n      - Binomial test vs H0=0.5\n      - Binomial test vs H0=0.191\n      \n4. **如果训练仍在运行**：报告当前进度和预估完成时间。\n\n5. **如果训练失败**：报告错误信息。\n\n### 产出\n写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_credit_agreement_results.md`\n\n### 服务器\n- bjb1-17606, account: root\n- 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`",

    "task_prompt": "## 任务：检查 binary reward credit agreement 训练是否完成，如完成则分析结果\n\n### 背景\n在 bjb1-17606 上用 nohup 启动了纯 binary reward (penalty=0) 的 Cell A (h=2) 训练。\n- 原始 PID: 14928（nohup bash wrapper）\n- Ray worker PID: 17165\n- 配置名：_alfworld_binary_credit_agreement\n- 训练日志：/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_training.log\n- Advantage 日志：/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl\n- 3 total training steps，预估 ~1.5-2 小时（从 17:42 开始，可能已完成）\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. **检查进程状态**（ssh_read_only）：\n   ```\n   ps -p 14928 -o pid,stat,etime 2>/dev/null || echo \"PROCESS_GONE\"\n   ```\n\n2. **检查训练日志末尾**：\n   ```\n   tail -50 /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_training.log\n   ```\n\n3. **如果训练已完成**：\n   a. 检查 advantage 日志是否存在：\n      ```\n      ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl\n      wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl\n      head -3 /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl\n      ```\n   \n   b. 如果 advantage 日志存在，用 Python 分析：\n      ```python\n      import json\n      import math\n      \n      # 读取数据\n      data = []\n      with open('/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl') as f:\n          for line in f:\n              data.append(json.loads(line))\n      \n      # 计算 credit agreement\n      # locally correct = window in successful episode with valid actions\n      # credit agreement = P(advantage > 0 | locally correct)\n      correct_sign = 0\n      total_correct_windows = 0\n      for entry in data:\n          if entry.get('episode_success', False):\n              for window in entry.get('windows', []):\n                  if window.get('valid_action', True):\n                      total_correct_windows += 1\n                      if window.get('advantage', 0) > 0:\n                          correct_sign += 1\n      \n      rate = correct_sign / total_correct_windows if total_correct_windows > 0 else 0\n      ```\n      \n      注意：日志格式可能不同，先用 head 查看实际格式再写分析代码。\n\n   c. 如果 advantage 日志不存在，检查 RAGEN 代码中 LOG_PER_SAMPLE_ADV 是否被支持：\n      ```\n      grep -r \"LOG_PER_SAMPLE_ADV\" /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN/\n      ```\n      如果不支持，尝试从 W&B 日志或训练输出中提取 advantage 数据。\n\n   d. 计算统计量：\n      - Credit agreement rate (%)\n      - 样本量 n\n      - Wilson 95% CI\n      - Binomial test vs H0=0.5\n      - Binomial test vs H0=0.191\n      \n4. **如果训练仍在运行**：报告当前进度和预估完成时间。\n\n5. **如果训练失败**：报告错误信息。\n\n### 产出\n写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_credit_agreement_results.md`\n\n### 服务器\n- bjb1-17606, account: root\n- 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`",

  "tools": "['Write']"

Window sweep 混用评估集**\n- 图标题写 \"OOD success\"，但 h=10 (6.25%=2/32) 和 h=20 (15.6%=5/32) 来自 32-task **ID** 集\n- episode (23.88%=32/134) 来自 134-task **OOD** 集\n- 同一图中混用不同评估集的指标，reviewer 会质疑\n- 建议: 修正图标题为 \"success rate\"，文本中注明 h≤20 为 ID 指标 / episode 为 OOD 指标；或补做 h=10/h=20 的 OOD eval\n\n### P1 — 应修复（3 个）\n\n1. **seed8 step5**: 论文 13.43% (18/134) vs registry 11.94% (16/134)，差 2 tasks\n2. **seed2048, seed42, seed2**: 3 个 seed 的 OOD eval 在 registry 中无可追溯条目（可能来自 RAGEN 内置 eval，未独立记录）\n3. **seed2 p=0.02**: 论文称 Fisher exact 但 p 值与 chi-squared (无 Yates) 吻合；Fisher exact 给 0.032 (two-sided) 或 0.016 (one-sided)\n\n### P2 — 可选修复（2 个）\n\n1. 8B Cell A \"1.5%\" 实为 1.5625% (1/64)\n2. r>0.99 episode correlation 声明无可追溯验证数据\n\n### 验证通过的部分\n\n- Table 1 全部核心数字 ✅\n- Wilson CI 12/12 全部在 ±0.2pp 内 ✅\n- Fisher 显著性方向 12/12 一致 ✅\n- Proposition 2 预测值 (19.1%/80.9%) ✅\n- Binomial test (p=0.025) ✅\n- ScienceWorld 全部 ✅\n- Abstract/Intro/Conclusion 引用数字 ✅\n\n---\n\n## Binary Reward 实验状态\n\nbjb1 上的纯 binary reward (penalty=0) 训练仍在运行（33% 完成，PID 14928）。但 Worker 发现**概念性问题**：\n\n- 实验用 γ=0.95，gamma-discounting 提供了隐式的 window 级 credit assignment\n- 成功 episode 中，task completion 之前的 window 获得正 advantage，之后的获得负 advantage\n- 导致 naive sign agreement = 100%（平凡结果）\n- **Proposition 2 假设 γ=1（所有 window 获得相同 episode-level advantage），当前实验无法验证 19.1% 预测**\n\n建议: 如需正确测试 Proposition 2，需设 γ=1.0 消除 gamma-discounting。但考虑到论文已有的 52% 数据（基于 penalty reward）足以支撑 sign misattribution diagnosis，是否值得追加 γ=1.0 实验请 Director 决策。"

[Director 指令] [Director 指令] 数据审计 P0 修复 + Binary Reward 分析纠正

## P0-1: seed5 数据错误修复

seed5 step10 论文写 25.37% (34/134) 但 Registry 验证为 29.10% (39/134)。Registry 有 verification note 确认 29.10% 正确。

**修复**：
1. Table 2 (experiments.tex): seed5 行 OOD 改为 29.10%，Wilson CI 改为 [22.1, 37.3]
2. experiments.tex §4.5 正文：OOD range 从 "23.88--30.60%" 更新（seed5 从 25.37→29.10 不影响 range 上下限，但确认 range 仍正确：seed1=23.88, seed4=30.60）
3. Table 7 (appendix.tex): 如果 step-5 unified 表有 seed5 数据也需更新
4. 检查 abstract/conclusion 中引用的 range 是否需要同步更新

## P0-2: Window sweep 混用评估集

**问题**：h=10 (6.25%=2/32) 和 h=20 (15.6%=5/32) 来自 32-task ID 集；episode (23.88%=32/134) 来自 134-task OOD 集。同一段/同一图混用不同评估集。

**修复方案**：
在 experiments.tex §4.3 window-size sweep 段落中明确标注评估集差异：
将 "$h{=}2$: 0\%, $h{=}5$: 0\%, $h{=}10$: 6.25\%, $h{=}20$: 15.6\%, episode-level: 23.88\%" 
改为 "$h{=}2$: 0\%, $h{=}5$: 0\%, $h{=}10$: 6.25\% (ID), $h{=}20$: 15.6\% (ID), episode-level: 23.88\% (OOD)"
并添加说明："The $h{\leq}20$ conditions were evaluated on the 32-task in-distribution set; the episode-level result is from the 134-task OOD set and thus not directly comparable in magnitude, though the qualitative threshold pattern is clear."

Figure 2(b) caption 也需注明评估集差异。

## P1 修复

1. **seed8 step5**: 如果 registry 确认 11.94%，更新 Table 2 和 Table 7 中 seed8 的值和 CI
2. **seed2 Fisher p-value**: 确认使用 two-sided Fisher exact test。如果 p=0.032 (two-sided)，将论文中 "p=0.02" 改为 "p=0.03"
3. **seed2048/42/2 不可追溯**: 在 Table 2 caption 或脚注说明这些 seed 使用 RAGEN built-in eval

## Binary Reward 实验：纠正错误分析

**你先前的分析（γ=0.95 使结果平凡）是错误的**。Reviewer 已纠正：

1. 相同的 γ=0.95 在 penalty reward 实验中只产生 52% sign-episode 相关性。如果 γ 是驱动因素，两个实验应给出相同结果。
2. 真正的驱动因素是 **binary reward 结构**：R ∈ {0, R+} 使 step_return 形成 clean 二元分割（fail=0, success>0），group mean 落在中间，所以 sign 完全由 episode outcome 决定。
3. Penalty reward 打破了这个结构（负 penalty 使 fail windows 也有非零 return），才导致相关性降到 ~52%。
4. **Step 1 全部 200 windows advantage=0（100% fail rate）是重要发现**：仅 1 步 sign-misattributed 训练即导致完全 collapse。

**不需要追加 γ=1.0 实验。** 当前 γ=0.95 的 binary reward 结果正是我们需要的——它验证了 binary reward 下 sign 完全由 episode outcome 决定，与 penalty reward 的 52% 形成对比，完美解释了 80.9% vs 52% 的 gap。

等实验跑完 3 步后，整合以下数据到论文：
1. binary reward sign-episode correlation = 100% (step 0)
2. step 1 后 success rate collapse to 0%
3. 三步总计的 credit agreement rate

把 P0/P1 修复和实验结果整合一起完成后汇报。

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	Step-independent training collapses while episode-level training succeeds; a factorial design separates the two mechanisms; and window size $h$ modulates severity.
5	
6	\subsection{Setup}
7	\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	We evaluate on ALFWorld~\cite{shridhar2021alfworld}, a text-based household environment with six task types requiring sequential reasoning over up to 50 steps. Reward is binary: $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. The base policy is Qwen3-1.7B fine-tuned on 2{,}546 expert demonstrations (SFT baseline: 12.5\% in-distribution, 0\% pre-SFT). At $p{=}0.125$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}80.9\%$ of gradient updates for locally correct windows carry incorrect sign. All conditions use RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} (v0.1, accessed May 2026)\footnote{Code available at \url{https://github.com/RAGEN-AI/RAGEN}.} with GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$, lr$=10^{-6}$, $\beta{=}0.01$, $\gamma{=}0.95$, 15 steps; full hyperparameters in Appendix~\ref{app:hyperparameters}). We report success rate on a 134-task OOD set (primary) and 32-task in-distribution set (secondary). Seed counts are noted per condition.
10	
11	\subsection{Factorial Mechanism Separation}
12	\label{sec:factorial_experiments}
13	
14	We implement the $2 \times 2$ factorial (Section~\ref{sec:factorial_design}): \textbf{Cell~A} (both mechanisms active; $h{=}2$, step-level advantage), \textbf{Cell~B} (M1 only; $h{=}2$, episode-level advantage), and \textbf{Cell~D} (neither; full trajectory, episode-level advantage). Two seeds each. The fourth cell---\textbf{Cell~C} (full observation, step-level advantage)---would isolate M2 without M1; we predict partial improvement over Cell~A, since removing information loss enables gradient alignment but step-level advantage may still introduce noise. We attempted this configuration but encountered persistent training instability (Appendix~\ref{app:cell_c}).
15

17	\title{Sign Misattribution in Step-Independent Agent RL:\\Dual Mechanism Diagnosis and Episode-Level Correction}
18	
19	\author{Anonymous}
20	
21	\begin{document}
22	\maketitle
23	
24	\begin{abstract}
25	Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning requires credit assignment across long interaction trajectories, yet current frameworks default to step-independent windowing that decomposes trajectories into independently optimized windows. This introduces a structural credit assignment pathology driven by two mechanisms: \textit{policy information loss}, which truncates context and imposes a constant performance ceiling, and \textit{advantage sign misattribution}, which causes gradient updates to reflect episode outcomes rather than local action quality, producing cumulative degradation. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with group size $K{=}8$, theory predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign under binary reward; empirical measurement under penalty reward yields a near-random 52\% credit agreement rate ($p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2pp effect size). A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms: eliminating sign misattribution converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning; removing information loss enables generalization. Episode-level Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), treating the full trajectory as a single optimization sample, addresses both structural preconditions. A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% of seeds avoid collapse, but only 36\% achieve statistically significant out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization ($p{<}0.01$; 
…[truncated 1400 chars]

_source line 3913 · 2026-05-23T11:39:27.471080+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Step-independent training in multi-turn agent RL fails through two mechanisms: policy information loss imposes a performance ceiling, while advantage sign misattribution causes cumulative degradation: at $p{=}12.5\%$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}$80.9\% incorrect-sign updates under binary reward (52\% measured under penalty reward, near-random credit assignment, $+$2pp above chance). A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms; episode-level GRPO addresses both (17.2\% mean OOD at 1.7B; preliminary single-seed 8B evidence shows 90.3\% OOD). A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% avoid collapse, but only 36\% generalize ($p{<}0.01$). The central insight---sign errors are more destructive than magnitude imprecision---applies to any agent RL method combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization.
5

8	Six contributing factors, from context length constraints to framework design inertia, are detailed in Appendix~\ref{app:why_defaults}.
9	
10	\paragraph{Seed-dependent OOD generalization.}
11	The multi-seed evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:seed_variability}) reveals a bimodal distribution: a stagnant cluster (10--16\% OOD, NS vs.\ SFT) and a generalized cluster (25--31\%, $p{<}0.001$). Training duration does not explain the gap (seed100: $+$1.50pp from step~5 to~10; seed5: 25.37\% at step~10), pointing to early training dynamics or initialization sensitivity. Identifying this mechanism is an important open question.
12	
13	\paragraph{Practitioner guidance.}
14	Use episode-level credit when the full episode fits in context; otherwise use the largest feasible $h$ ($h{\geq}20$; $h{\leq}10$ collapses). Finer-grained methods (Turn-PPO, GAGPO) should be applied only after establishing an episode-level baseline, as they assume correct gradient direction.
15	
16

56	\paragraph{Window-size sweep.} A credit assignment threshold between $h{=}10$ and $h{=}20$ separates collapse from recovery (Figure~\ref{fig:hsweep}b; seed~1): $h{=}2$: 0\%, $h{=}5$: 0\%, $h{=}10$: 6.25\% (ID), $h{=}20$: 15.6\% (ID), episode-level: 23.88\% (OOD). The $h{\leq}20$ conditions were evaluated on the 32-task in-distribution set; the episode-level result is from the 134-task OOD set and thus not directly comparable in magnitude, though the qualitative threshold pattern is clear. This threshold is observed in a single seed; multi-seed verification is needed to confirm its robustness.% exp_id: h_sweep_series, exp029_seed1
57	\label{sec:results_hsweep}
58	
59	\subsection{Sign Misattribution Verification}
60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% ($n{=}3{,}000$ windows; 95\% CI [50.3\%, 53.9\%]) under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), marginally above random chance ($p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2.07pp effect size). Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver. This operationalization is a conservative approximation: successful episodes may contain suboptimal windows, making our label an upper bound on true local correctness. Even under this generous criterion, credit agreement remains near-random, strengthening the diagnosis.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63	
64	\subsection{Episode-Level Training: Seed Variability}
65	\label{sec:seed_variability}
66
