SSH 到 bjb1-17606，检查 PID 14928 是否还在运行：\n   ```\n   ps -p 14928 -o pid,stat,etime,args 2>/dev/null || echo \"PROCESS_ENDED\"\n   ```\n\n2. 检查训练日志：\n   ```\n   tail -50 /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_training.log\n   ```\n\n3. 如果训练完成，收集关键数据：\n   a. advantage 日志总行数：`wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl`\n   b. 用 Python 分析 credit agreement：\n   ```python\n   import json\n   \n   records = []\n   with open('/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl') as f:\n       for line in f:\n           records.append(json.loads(line))\n   \n   # 按 iteration 分组统计\n   # 每条记录有: episode_id, window_position, step_return, advantage, uid, window_action_valid, window_action_valid_ratio\n   \n   # 关键分析：\n   # 1. 每个 iteration 的 episode success rate (step_return > 0 的 episode 比例)\n   # 2. sign(advantage) vs sign(step_return > 0) 的 agreement rate (整体和分 iteration)\n   # 3. 训练 step 0 (iter 0) 的 success rate\n   # 4. 后续 iteration 的 success rate（是否 collapse）\n   # 5. 零 advantage 比例（all-fail groups）\n   \n   # 注意：binary reward 下 step_return 要么 >0 (成功 episode 的 window)，要么 =0 (失败 episode)\n   # 所以 sign(advantage) 理论上 100% 由 episode outcome 决定（除了 all-fail groups 的零 advantage）\n   \n   print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n   \n   # 分 iteration\n   from collections import defaultdict\n   iter_records = defaultdict(list)\n   for r in records:\n       # episode_id 可以推断 iteration：每个 iteration 有不同的 episode_id 范围\n       iter_records[r.get('iteration', -1)].append(r)\n   \n   for it, recs in sorted(iter_records.items()):\n       n = len(recs)\n       # success = step_return > 0\n       success_windows = sum(1 for r in recs if r['step_return'] > 0)\n       fail_windows = sum(1 for r in recs if r['step_return'] == 0)\n       zero_adv = sum(1 for r in recs if r['advantage'] == 0)\n       \n       # credit agreement: sign(advantage) matches sign(step_return > 0)\n       agree = 0\n       disagree = 0\n       for r in recs:\n           if r['advantage'] == 0:\n               continue  # skip zero advantage (no gradient)\n           is_success = r['step_return'] > 0\n           adv_positive = r['advantage'] > 0\n           if is_success == adv_positive:\n               agree += 1\n           else:\n               disagree += 1\n       \n       total_nonzero = agree + disagree\n       agreement_rate = agree / total_nonzero if total_nonzero > 0 else float('nan')\n       \n       print(f\"\\nIteration {it}: {n} records\")\n       print(f\"  Success windows: {success_windows}, Fail windows: {fail_windows}\")\n       print(f\"  Zero advantage: {zero_adv}\")\n       print(f\"  Credit agreement: {agree}/{total_nonzero} = {agreement_rate:.4f}\" if total_nonzero > 0 else \"  No non-zero advantages\")\n   ```\n\n   c. 也计算总体 credit agreement\n   d. 检查 W&B run metrics：训练 loss、KL、reward 等\n\n4. 如果训练未完成，报告进度和预估完成时间。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器: bjb1-17606\n- 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n\n### 产出\n将结果写到 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_reward_final_results.md`",

  "tools": "['Write']"
}

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Generality and mechanism interaction.}
5	Sign misattribution arises whenever three structural properties co-occur: (a) step-level windowing, (b) sparse binary reward, and (c) group-relative normalization. Under these conditions, the advantage sign collapses to a proxy for episode outcome regardless of local quality, and severity scales with task difficulty ($P(\text{correct} \mid \text{update}) \to p$). M1 and M2 interact in a vicious cycle: M1 lowers $p$ by preventing multi-step strategies, increasing wrong-sign updates under M2, which further degrades $p$, explaining why step-independent training produces active collapse rather than slow convergence. The A$\to$B comparison provides direct evidence for M2: both conditions use identical observation scope ($h{=}2$), differing only in advantage granularity. Eliminating step-level advantage converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning. Cell~C would provide independent confirmation of M2 in isolation; its consistent training instability across six hyperparameter configurations (Appendix~\ref{app:cell_c}) prevented this validation.
6	
7	\paragraph{Why step-independent became the default.}
8	Six contributing factors, from context length constraints to framework design inertia, are detailed in Appendix~\ref{app:why_defaults}.
9	
10	\paragraph{Seed-dependent OOD generalization.}
11	The multi-seed evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:seed_variability}) reveals a bimodal distribution: a stagnant cluster (10--16\% OOD, NS vs.\ SFT) and a generalized cluster (24--31\%, $p{<}0.001$). Training duration does not explain the gap (seed100: $+$1.50pp from step~5 to~10; seed5: 29.10\% at step~10), pointing to early training dynamics or initialization sensitivity. Identifying this mechanism is an important open question.
12

14	Use episode-level credit when the full episode fits in context; otherwise use the largest feasible $h$ ($h{\g

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{RL for multi-turn LLM agents.}
5	Policy gradient methods---PPO~\cite{schulman2017ppo}, GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, DPO~\cite{rafailov2023dpo}---underpin RLHF~\cite{ouyang2022instructgpt} and have been extended to multi-turn agents~\cite{yao2023react} through step-independent windowing~\cite{ragen2025}. Subsequent work improves algorithms (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}), reward design (Self-Guide~\cite{selfguide2026}), or training pipelines (SPEAR~\cite{spear2025}, DigiRL~\cite{digiRL2024}), but all share a common assumption: trajectory decomposition into fixed windows is benign. We show this assumption is false---the windowing introduces a structural credit assignment pathology that persists under default configurations.
6	
7	\paragraph{Credit assignment in reinforcement learning.}
8	TD($\lambda$)~\cite{sutton1988td}, GAE~\cite{schulman2016gae}, and TRPO~\cite{schulman2015trpo} interpolate between bootstrapping and Monte Carlo returns; REINFORCE~\cite{williams1992reinforce} uses full episode returns. Reward shaping~\cite{ng1999shaping} and hindsight experience replay~\cite{andrychowicz2017her} address sparse reward in classical RL; counterfactual baselines~\cite{foerster2018coma} handle team reward credit assignment. Process reward models~\cite{lightman2024lets,wang2024mathshepherd} provide step-level supervision for reasoning tasks. In the low-success-rate regime ($p < 0.2$) typical of agent tasks, the classical bias-variance tradeoff breaks down: Proposition~2 shows sign errors under windowing grow as $1{-}p$, dominating any variance reduction that windowing provides.
9	
10	\paragraph{Credit assignment and sign misattribution evidence.}
11	Several independent studies corroborate our diagnosis. IRC~\cite{irc2026} reports per-turn advantages frequently disagree in sign with outcome advantages. MC-GRPO~\cite{mcgrpo2025} measures a 5\% sign-flip rate. GRPO-$\lambda$~\cite{grpolambda2025} proves val
…[truncated 2162 chars]

2

9	## Key Finding
10	
11	**Credit agreement = 100% (157/157)** among non-zero advantage windows.
12	
13	With binary reward (penalty=0), sign(advantage) perfectly matches sign(step_return > 0). [Director 指令] [Director 指令] Binary reward 数据整合到 Section 4.4

## 核心任务
在 experiments.tex Section 4.4 (Sign Misattribution Verification) 末尾，添加一段 binary reward 验证结果。使用 step 0 已有数据。

## 数据（来自 iteration 0）
- 实验设置：pure binary reward（penalty=0），h=2，γ=0.95，K=8
- Sign-episode correlation: **100%**（157/157 非零 advantage windows）
- 零 advantage（all-fail groups）: 243/400 = 60.75%
- Episode success rate: 12/72 = 16.67%

## 关键 framing（必须严格遵守）
- **不要**用 "credit agreement" 描述 100%
- **使用** "sign-episode correlation"：advantage sign 完全由 episode outcome 决定
- 100% sign-episode correlation 是 **sign misattribution (M2) 的直接证据**：在 binary reward 下，sign 完全追踪 outcome 而非 local quality
- 与 penalty reward 的 52% 对比要说明：penalty reward 引入 within-group variance 部分打破了这个完美相关，但 sign 仍然主要由 outcome 决定（52% ≈ random for local quality）
- 60.75% all-fail groups 验证了 reward saturation（Section 3: $(1-p)^K ≈ 34.4\%$ 理论值 vs 60.75% 实测——差异因为 binary 没有 penalty 的 partial reward）

## 写作要求
- 1 段，~4-5 句话
- 不要超过现有段落长度
- 改完后编译确认主文仍 ≤8 页
- 注意：新增文本在 Section 4.4 末尾（§4.4 当前在第 6 页），不会影响第 8 页分页

_source line 3993 · 2026-05-23T11:58:47.563219+00:00_

59	\subsection{Sign Misattribution Verification}
60	\label{sec:sign_verification}
61	
62	We operationalize local correctness (Definition~1) through episode outcome: in a successful episode, all windows with valid actions are labeled locally correct. The credit agreement rate---the fraction of such windows where GRPO's advantage sign is positive---is 52.07\% ($n{=}3{,}000$ windows; 95\% CI [50.3\%, 53.9\%]) under step-independent training ($h{=}2$), marginally above random chance ($p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2.07pp effect size). Advantage sign correlates near-perfectly with episode outcome ($r{>}0.99$) but shows near-zero dependence on local quality (point biserial $r{=}0.068$). Combined with $\gamma{=}1.0$ collapse (Appendix~\ref{app:additional}), these measurements confirm sign misattribution as the causal driver. This operationalization is a conservative approximation: successful episodes may contain suboptimal windows, making our label an upper bound on true local correctness. Even under this generous criterion, credit agreement remains near-random, strengthening the diagnosis.% exp_id: 7b_cell_a_h2
63	
64	\subsection{Episode-Level Training: Seed Variability}
65	\label{sec:seed_variability}
66	
67	Episode-level GRPO addresses the dual-mechanism failure, but training outcomes remain seed-dependent. We systematically evaluate $N{=}11$ random seeds under identical hyperparameters, revealing a $2{\times}2$ structure: \textit{training stability} (collapsed vs.\ non-collapsed) $\times$ \textit{OOD generalization} (stagnant vs.\ generalized).
68

## 全局样式规范（所有图必须遵守）

import numpy as np

plt.rcParams.update({

})

# 项目统一配色（全文一致）
COLORS = {
    'cell_a': '#D62728',   # 红 — Cell A (M1+M2, collapse)
    'cell_b': '#FF7F0E',   # 橙 — Cell B (M1 only, bounded)
    'cell_c': '#AAAAAA',   # 灰 — Cell C (unstable)
    'cell_d': '#1F77B4',   # 蓝 — Cell D (neither, success)
    'sft':    '#7F7F7F',   # 灰 — SFT baseline
    'binary': '#2C3E50',   # 深蓝灰 — Binary reward
    'penalty':'#E74C3C',   # 红 — Penalty reward
    'success':'#27AE60',   # 绿 — Success/positive
    'fail':   '#E74C3C',   # 红 — Fail/negative
    'zero':   '#BDC3C7',   # 浅灰 — Zero gradient
    'gen':    '#2ECC71',   # 亮绿 — Generalized seeds
    'stag':   '#F39C12',   # 橙 — Stagnant seeds
    'coll':   '#E74C3C',   # 红 — Collapsed seeds
}
```

**不要用 `text.usetex=True`**（本地无完整 TeX）。用 matplotlib mathtext（`$...$`）渲染公式。

## 任务 1: 新 Figure — Overview Concept Figure

**位置**：论文 Figure 1（放在 Introduction 或 Method 开头，`\textwidth`，2 栏宽）
**narrative_intent**: 让读者 30 秒理解 step-independent vs episode-level 的核心差异和双机制
**内容**：
- **左半**: Step-independent windowing 示意
  - 一个长 trajectory 被切成 h-step windows（用不同色块表示）
  - 每个 window 独立获得 advantage（标注 $\hat{A}_k$）
  - 标注 M1（"truncated context"）和 M2（"sign = episode outcome"）
  - 一个 fail episode 示意：所有 window 得到负 advantage（即使某些 action 是 locally correct）
- **右半**: Episode-level 方案
  - 整个 trajectory 作为一个 unit
  - 一个 advantage 覆盖全程（标注 $\hat{A}_{episode}$）
  - 标注 "full context" + "sign = episode outcome (correct)"
- **风格**: 干净的示意图，用 matplotlib patches/arrows，不是数据图表。颜色用上面的 COLORS。

**文件**: `fig_overview.pdf`，放在 paper 目录

## 任务 2: 新 Figure — Sign Misattribution Visualization

**位置**: Section 4.4 附近，`\columnwidth`（单栏宽）
**narrative_intent**: 直观展示 binary reward 下 sign 完全由 episode outcome 决定 vs penalty reward 下接近随机
**数据**:
- Binary reward (step 0): 157 non-zero windows → 100% sign-episode correlation; 243 zero (all-fail)

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Problem Formulation and GRPO}
5	\label{sec:problem_formulation}
6	
7	We model the multi-turn agent as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) where the policy $\pi_\phi(a_t \mid h_t)$ generates actions conditioned on interaction history $h_t = (o_1, a_1, \ldots, o_t)$. Episodes of $T$ steps terminate with binary reward $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} samples $K$ rollouts per task and computes group-relative advantages:
8	\begin{align}
9	\hat{A}_k &= \frac{R_k - \mu}{\sigma + \epsilon}, \notag \\
10	\mu &= \frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K R_j, \quad \sigma = \sqrt{\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K (R_j - \mu)^2}
11	\label{eq:grpo_advantage}
12	\end{align}
13	GRPO is episode-level by design: each rollout receives one return and one advantage, with no inherent temporal discounting.
14	
15	\subsection{Step-Independent Windowing}
16	\label{sec:step_independent}
17	
18	Agent RL frameworks must reconcile GRPO's episode-level design with the memory constraints of long trajectories. RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} introduces \textbf{step-independent training}: the trajectory is partitioned into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each receiving a discounted return:
19	\begin{equation}
20	R_k = \gamma^{(N - k)} \cdot R_{\text{episode}}
21	\label{eq:step_return}
22	\end{equation}
23	where $N = \lceil T/h \rceil$ is the number of windows. Each window is optimized as an independent GRPO sample. This reduces sequence length from $O(T)$ to $O(h)$ and multiplies the effective batch size, but structurally alters credit assignment.
24	
25	\subsection{Dual Mechanism Analysis}
26	\label{sec:dual_mechanism}
27	
28	Two mechanisms jointly cause training failure under step-independent optimization.
29	
30	\subsubsection{Mechanism 1: Policy Information Loss}
31	\label{sec:m1_information_loss}
32	
33	Step-independent training truncates context at both inference and training time. The policy $\pi_\phi(a_t \mid h_{\text{window}_k})
…[truncated 2758 chars]

1	\section{Theoretical Analysis}
2	\label{sec:theory}
3	
4	Sign misattribution is the more fundamental failure mechanism because it causes active divergence, not merely slow convergence. We derive the probability of incorrect gradient direction below; full proofs appear in Appendix~\ref{app:theory_full}.
5	
6	\textbf{Definition 1} (Locally correct action). \textit{An action $a_t$ is \textbf{locally correct} if it would receive positive advantage under an oracle that observes the full episode history $h_t$ and has access to the true action-value function. Equivalently, window $k$ contains locally correct actions if replacing them (holding all other windows fixed) would decrease the probability of episode success.}
7	
8	\textbf{Proposition 1.} \textit{Under step-independent training with binary episode reward $R_{\text{episode}} \in \{0, r_+\}$, the probability that window $k$'s advantage $\hat{A}_k$ has the correct sign (aligned with local action quality) approaches the base success rate $p$ as the number of rollouts $K$ per group increases.}
9	
10	\textit{Proof sketch.} In a failed episode ($R_{\text{episode}} = 0$), all windows receive $R_k = 0$. If any rollout in the group succeeded, $\mu > 0$, so every window in the failed episode receives $\hat{A}_k < 0$---including windows with locally correct actions. The probability of receiving the wrong sign in a failed episode is $1 - (1-p)^{K-1}$ (at least one group member succeeded). As $K$ grows, this approaches $1-p$, and the overall probability of correct sign approaches $p$. \hfill$\square$
11	
12	The key distinction is temporal. Information loss (M1) imposes a constant ceiling determined by $h$; sign misattribution (M2) is cumulative---each wrong-sign gradient step erodes policy competence below that ceiling. A wrong-sign gradient moves the optimizer \textit{away} from good policies; a reduced-magnitude gradient from discounting ($\gamma^{(N-k)}$) still points toward good policies. Sign errors produce active diverge

So:\n  - \"Sign = episode outcome\" (深蓝灰) = 157 (100% of non-zero)\n  - \"Zero gradient\" (浅灰) = 243\n\n  Better framing: \n  - Sign tracks episode outcome: 157/157 = 100%\n  - No gradient signal: 243/400 = 60.75%\n\n**Penalty Reward 侧 (右):**\n- \"Sign = episode outcome\" ≈ 52% of 3000 = 1562\n- \"Sign ≠ episode outcome\" ≈ 48% of 3000 = 1438\n- Zero gradient: much smaller fraction (not measured exactly, assume negligible)\n\n**Alternative simpler design (preferred):**\nTwo simple bars showing \"Sign-Episode Agreement Rate\":\n- Binary: 100% (157/157)\n- Penalty: 52.07% (1562/3000)\n- 50% random chance reference line (dashed gray)\n- Annotate the binary bar: \"n=157 (60.8% zero-gradient excluded)\"\n- Annotate the penalty bar: \"n=3,000\"\n\nThis is cleaner. K=4: 蓝 (#1F77B4), solid\n  - K=8: 红 (#D62728), solid, slightly thicker (这是论文的设置)\n  - K=16: 绿 (#2CA02C), solid\n  - K→∞ (y=p): 灰 (#7F7F7F), dashed, label \"$K \\\\to \\\\infty$\"\n\n**关键标注:**\n- Shaded region: p < 0.2 (典型 agent task range)，用浅灰色填充，边界用 dashed line\n- 操作点 p=0.125, K=8: 大号红色圆点标记 + annotation box 指向它，文字 \"ALFWorld\\n$p$=12.5%, $K$=8\\n$P_c$=19.1%\"\n- 50% random chance 参考线: 水平灰色虚线 at y=0.5，标注 \"random chance\"\n- 可选: penalty reward 实测点 at (p=0.125, y=0.52) 标为五角星，label \"penalty reward (measured)\"\n\n**注释框样式:**\n```python\nbbox_props = dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='lightyellow', edgecolor='gray', alpha=0.9)\narrowprops = dict(arrowstyle='->', connectionstyle='arc3,rad=0.2', color='gray')\n```\n\n### 全局样式\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 2.0,\n    'axes.grid': True,\n    'grid.alpha': 0.3,\n    'grid.linestyle': '--',\n    'legend.framealpha': 0.8,\n    'legend.edgecolor': 'none',\n})\n```\n\n**不要用 `text.usetex=True`**。用 matplotlib mathtext。\n\n### 验证\n生成后 `ls -la` 确认文件。",

Reviewer 已审查完成的图表，发现以下需要修正的问题。**在匿名仓库任务之前完成。**

## 1. fig_overview 术语修正（3 处）

重新生成 fig_overview.pdf，修正以下标签：
- **右侧标题** "Episode-Level Verification" → **"Episode-Level Training"**
- **底部 M2 标签** "M2: reward divergence" → **"M2: sign misattribution"**
- **底部 M1 标签** "M1: perf. ceiling" → **"M1: information loss"**

其余设计不变（Reviewer 确认视觉质量好、配色正确、整体设计清晰）。

## 2. fig_training_dynamics Panel (b) — ID/OOD 区分更显著

当前底部有小字注释 ID/OOD 区别，但太不显著。改为：
- 在 h≤20 的 bar 上方数值旁加 **(ID)** 标注
- 在 Full bar 上方数值旁加 **(OOD)** 标注
- 或者用不同色调/边框区分两种 eval protocol

## 3. fig_sign_misattribution — 零梯度率修正

当前注释写 "60.8% of windows have zero gradient (excluded)" 不准确。
- 如果图只展示 step 0 数据（n=157），零梯度率应为 **21.5%**（43/200 windows at step 0）
- 60.75% = (43+200)/400 是跨 step 0+1 的混合数字
- 修改为 "21.5% zero-gradient windows excluded (step 0)" 或注明计算跨越了 steps

## 4. experiments.tex — RAGEN commit hash

experiments.tex 第 9 行 "(v0.1, accessed May 2026)" 需要加上 git commit hash 以满足可复现性。
SSH 到 bjb1 查看 `/root/cost-verify-agent-rl` 的 RAGEN 依赖版本（`pip show ragen` 或 `git log` 在 ragen 目录），获取 commit hash 后更新为 "(v0.1, commit \texttt{abc1234}, accessed May 2026)"。

## 完成后
编译验证主文 ≤8 页，然后汇报。之后再处理匿名仓库任务。

_source line 4035 · 2026-05-23T12:59:51.377648+00:00_

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	Step-independent training collapses while episode-level training succeeds; a factorial design separates the two mechanisms; and window size $h$ modulates severity.
5	
6	\subsection{Setup}
7	\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	We evaluate on ALFWorld~\cite{shridhar2021alfworld}, a text-based household environment with six task types requiring sequential reasoning over up to 50 steps. Reward is binary: $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. The base policy is Qwen3-1.7B fine-tuned on 2{,}546 expert demonstrations (SFT baseline: 12.5\% in-distribution, 0\% pre-SFT). At $p{=}0.125$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}80.9\%$ of gradient updates for locally correct windows carry incorrect sign. All conditions use RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} (v0.1, accessed May 2026)\footnote{Code available at \url{https://github.com/RAGEN-AI/RAGEN}.} with GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$, lr$=10^{-6}$, $\beta{=}0.01$, $\gamma{=}0.95$, 15 steps; full hyperparameters in Appendix~\ref{app:hyperparameters}). We report success rate on a 134-task OOD set (primary) and 32-task in-distribution set (secondary). Seed counts are noted per condition.
10	
11	\subsection{Factorial Mechanism Separation}
12	\label{sec:factorial_experiments}

## 仓库信息
- 仓库地址: https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026-2/cost-verify-agent-rl
- Push URL (带 token): https://[REDACTED]@github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026-2/cost-verify-agent-rl.git

## 代码来源
远程服务器 bjb1-17606 上的 `/root/cost-verify-agent-rl`

## 操作步骤

### 1. 从 bjb1 拉取代码
SSH 到 bjb1，打包需要的文件（.py, .sh, configs, requirements），排除以下内容：
- 实验日志、checkpoints、模型权重
- wandb/ 目录
- data/ 目录（或大数据文件）
- .agent-ml-research-* 元数据（registry.yaml, decisions.yaml, dream_*, mistakes.yaml, AGENTS.md, ROADMAP.md 等）
- .env、任何 token/密钥文件
- __pycache__、.pyc

### 2. 匿名化（必须）
替换以下敏感信息（代码、注释、config 中全部搜索替换）：
- `caoxiaoyu` → `anonymous`
- `caoxiaoyuyuyuyuyu` → `anonymous`  
- `1844118046@qq.com` → `anonymous@example.com`
- 任何真实姓名、机构名、wandb entity 名
- 检查 README 中是否有身份信息

### 3. 创建干净仓库
在本地创建新 git repo：
```bash
git init
git config user.name "Anonymous"
git config user.email "anonymous@example.com"
```
- 添加所有清理后的文件
- 写一个匿名化的 README.md：项目说明、环境配置、如何运行实验
- 保留 requirements.txt / setup 脚本确保可复现
- 单次 commit（不保留历史）

### 4. Push
```bash
git remote add origin https://[REDACTED]@github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026-2/cost-verify-agent-rl.git
git push -u origin main
```

### 5. 验证
- 确认 push 成功
- 检查仓库中无敏感信息泄露
- 汇报：repo URL、文件数、commit 数

**注意**：token 仅用于 push，不要写入任何文件或 commit 中。Push 完成后清理本地 remote URL。

{
  "task_type": "figure:theory_curve_v2",
  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：美化 Theory Curve 图（fig_theory_curve.pdf）\n\n生成 `fig_theory_curve.pdf`，放在 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/docs/paper/fig_theory_curve.pdf`\n\n### 内容\nX 轴: Episode success rate p (0 to 1)\nY 轴: P(correct sign | update) (0 to 1)\n\n### 数据（从 Proposition 2 公式计算）\nP(correct | update) = p / (1 - (1-p)^K)\n\n画 4 条曲线:\n- K=4: 蓝色 '#1F77B4', linewidth=2\n- K=8: 红色 '#D62728', linewidth=2.5 (稍粗，因为这是我们实验用的 K)\n- K=16: 绿色 '#2CA02C', linewidth=2\n- K→∞ (即 y=p): 灰色虚线 '#7F7F7F', linewidth=1.5\n\n### 标注\n1. **操作点 p=12.5%**: 在 K=8 曲线上标记一个大号红色圆点 (s=100)，加注释框:\n   - 文字: \"$p=12.5\\\\%$, $K=8$\\n$P_c = 19.1\\\\%$\"\n   - 用 annotate + arrowprops 连接到点\n   - 注释框用白色背景 + 浅灰边框\n\n2. **Shaded region** \"Typical agent task range\": p ∈ [0, 0.2]\n   - 用浅灰色 fill_between，alpha=0.15\n   - 边界用 dashed line\n\n3. **50% random chance 参考线**: 水平虚线 y=0.5，灰色，标注 \"random chance\"\n\n4. **可选 penalty reward 实测点**: 在 p=0.125 处画一个菱形标记，y=0.52 (52% credit agreement under penalty reward)，用不同颜色（橙色），标注 \"penalty reward (measured)\"\n\n### 全局样式\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 2.0,\n    'axes.grid': True,\n    'grid.alpha': 0.3,\n    'grid.linestyle': '--',\n    'legend.framealpha': 0.8,\n    'legend.edgecolor': 'none',\n})\n```\n\n**不要用 `text.usetex=True`**。用 matplotlib mathtext（`$...$`）渲染公式。\n\n### 图尺寸\n单栏宽（`\\columnwidth`），约 3.5 × 2.8 inches\n\n### 验证\n生成后 `ls -la` 确认文件。",
  "tools": "['Bash', 'Write', 'Read']"
}

_source line 4090 · 2026-05-23T13:23:09.437703+00:00_

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	Step-independent training collapses while episode-level training succeeds; a factorial design separates the two mechanisms; and window size $h$ modulates severity.
5	
6	\subsection{Setup}
7	\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	We evaluate on ALFWorld~\cite{shridhar2021alfworld}, a text-based household environment with six task types requiring sequential reasoning over up to 50 steps. Reward is binary: $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. The base policy is Qwen3-1.7B fine-tuned on 2{,}546 expert demonstrations (SFT baseline: 12.5\% in-distribution, 0\% pre-SFT). At $p{=}0.125$ with $K{=}8$, Proposition~2 predicts ${\sim}80.9\%$ of gradient updates for locally correct windows carry incorrect sign. All conditions use RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} (v0.1, commit \texttt{3a6b48b})\footnote{Our implementation extends RAGEN v0.1 with episode-level training support. Code: \url{https://github.com/RAGEN-AI/RAGEN}.} with GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$, lr$=10^{-6}$, $\beta{=}0.01$, $\gamma{=}0.95$, 15 steps; full hyperparameters in Appendix~\ref{app:hyperparameters}). We report success rate on a 134-task OOD set (primary) and 32-task in-distribution set (secondary). Seed counts are noted per condition.
10	
11	\subsection{Factorial Mechanism Separation}
12	\label{sec:factorial_experiments}
13	
14	We implement the $2 \times 2$ factorial (Section~\ref{sec:factorial_design}): \textbf{Cell~A} (both mechanisms active; $h{=}2$, step-level advantage), \textbf{Cell~B} (M1 only; $h{=}2$, episode-level advantage), and \textbf{Cell~D} (neither; full trajectory, episode-level advantage). Two seeds each. The fourth cell---\textbf{Cell~C} (full observation, step-level advantage)---would isolate M2 without M1; we predict partial improvement over Cell~A, since removing information loss enables gradient alignment but step-level advantage may still introduce noise. We attempted this configuration but encountered persistent training instability (Appendix~\ref{app:cell_

18	
19	\author{Anonymous}

21	\begin{document}
22	\maketitle
23	
24	\begin{abstract}
25	Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning requires credit assignment across long interaction trajectories, yet current frameworks default to step-independent windowing that decomposes trajectories into independently optimized windows. This introduces a structural credit assignment pathology driven by two mechanisms: \textit{policy information loss}, which truncates context and imposes a constant performance ceiling, and \textit{advantage sign misattribution}, which causes gradient updates to reflect episode outcomes rather than local action quality, producing cumulative degradation. At $p{=}12.5\%$ with group size $K{=}8$, theory predicts 80.9\% of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign under binary reward; empirical measurement under penalty reward yields a near-random 52\% credit agreement rate ($p{=}0.025$ vs.\ 50\%; $+$2pp effect size). A $2{\times}2$ factorial separates the mechanisms: eliminating sign misattribution converts catastrophic collapse into bounded learning; removing information loss enables generalization. Episode-level Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), treating the full trajectory as a single optimization sample, addresses both structural preconditions. A systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals that stable training is necessary but not sufficient: 73\% of seeds avoid collapse, but only 36\% achieve statistically significant out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization ($p{<}0.01$; 
…[truncated 1672 chars]

[Director 指令] ## Round 3 审稿修复 — DDL 2026-05-25（~36h）

Round 3 评分 Overall 3.0 (Findings)，目标通过修复冲刺 3.5+。以下分三个阶段，Phase 1 立即执行，Phase 2 并行。

---

### Phase 1: 写作修复（所有 .tex 文件）

#### 1.1 Abstract (`main.tex`)
- "eliminates both failure modes" → "addresses both failure modes"
- 确认 abstract ≤ 200 words

#### 1.2 Introduction (`introduction.tex`)
- **Contribution 结构调整**：把 Contribution 3（episode-level GRPO correction + OOD improvement）合并到 Contribution 2 中。Episode-level GRPO 不是新方法（它是 GRPO 原始设计），不能作为独立贡献。合并后只保留 2 个 Contribution。
- Contribution 2 合并后大意："We validate this diagnosis through a 2×2 factorial experiment... episode-level training (reverting to GRPO's original design) addresses both mechanisms and yields XX% OOD improvement at 1.7B scale, with preliminary evidence at 8B."
- "causes the majority of gradient updates...to carry incorrect sign"：在 penalty reward 下 credit agreement 52%（约一半），不是 majority。改为 "causes a substantial fraction of gradient updates to carry incorrect sign" 或类似表述
- "substantial OOD improvement at both 1.7B and 8B scales"：8B 是单 seed，必须加 "preliminary" 修饰

#### 1.3 Theoretical Analysis (`theoretical_analysis.tex`)
- **Proposition 2**（line 14-18）：19.1% 改为 "upper bound"。当前写法将其作为精确值，但 Proposition 隐含假设 episode 成功⟹所有窗口局部正确，这只是上界。改为：
  - "P(correct|update) ≤ p/(1-(1-p)^K)"
  - "yielding an upper bound of 19.1% at p=12.5%, K=8"
- **Eq.6**（line 35-40 附近）：1.32 ratio 是单点测量（单 seed、单训练步）。加 caveat："This ratio is measured from a single training snapshot; broader validation across seeds and training stages would strengthen this estimate."

#### 1.4 Experiments (`experiments.tex`)
- **Section 4.1**（line 9 附近）："Reward is binary: R ∈ {0, r+}" 与实际使用 penalty reward（-0.1 per invalid action）矛盾。修复：明确说明主实验使用 penalty reward structure（R ∈ {-0.1, 0, r+}），理论分析假设 binary reward（R ∈ {0, r+}），并解释两者差异如何影响预测值。
- **52% credit agreement 重新框架化**（line 62 附近）：必须清晰区分三个指标：
  1. **Sign-episode correlation**：advantage sign 是否跟随 episode outcome（成功/失败）。Binary reward 下 100%（by construction，因为 gamma discounting 使所有成功 episode 窗口 return > 0）
  2. **Credit agreement**：advantage sign 是否匹配 local action quality（action validity）。Penalty reward 下测得 52%
  3. **P(correct|update)**：Proposition 2 的理论指标，预测上界 19.1%
  
  当前论文混淆了这三个概念。修改要点：
  - 52% credit agreement 说明 advantage sign 几乎不携带 local action quality 信息（接近随机 50%）
  - Binary reward 100% sign-episode correlation 证明 M2（advantage sign 完全由 episode outcome 决定），而不是验证 Proposition 2 的 19.1%
  - 19.1% 是 P(correct|update) 的理论上界，与 52% credit agreement 是不同指标，不能直接比较

- **Table 1**（line 33-52）：
  - Cell D 的 11-seed study 已完成（Table 2）。在 Table 1 caption 或脚注中引用："Cell D results are consistent with the 11-seed evaluation (Table 2), where mean OOD = 17.2% with 36% seed generalization rate."
  - 明确标注每个 cell 的 seed 数量和报告标准

- **Cell C 重新框架化**：Cell C 的 NaN instability 与 Cell A 的 gradual collapse 是质性不同的失败模式。这不是单纯的缺失数据——它暗示 M1 可能有稳定化作用（限制梯度方差），M1-M2 可能存在非加性交互。在 experiments 相关段落（或 Discussion）添加这一分析。

#### 1.5 Discussion (`discussion.tex`)
- **新增 M1 稳定化假设段**：Cell C (full trajectory + step-level advantage) 出现 NaN instability，而非 Cell A 的 gradual collapse。这暗示：
  - M1（information loss from windowing）可能有意外的梯度方差限制作用
  - M1-M2 交互可能是非加性的——消除 M1 但保留 M2 可能释放不稳定的梯度信号
  - 这限制了论文的 "additive separation" 假设的适用范围
  - 措辞用 hypothesis/suggests，不过度声明

- **HCAPO 分析预测段**（见 Phase 2 调研结果后补充）

#### 1.6 Conclusion (`conclusion.tex`)
- "applies to any agent RL method" → 缩窄到 "applies to methods combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization"

#### 1.7 引用修复 (`references.bib`)
- Turn-PPO：核实 arXiv ID 是否正确，修正
- MC-GRPO：核实论文标题是否正确，修正
- IRC：核实作者名拼写，修正
- **新增引用**：
  - Harutyunyan et al. 2019 "Hindsight Credit Assignment" 
  - Arjona-Medina et al. 2019 "RUDDER: Return Decomposition for Delayed Rewards"
  - 至少 1 篇 POMDP 经典文献（建议 Kaelbling et al. 1998）
  - 在 Related Work 或 Discussion 中引用这三篇，说明与本文 sign misattribution 诊断的关系

#### 1.8 匿名性修复
- Page 5 脚注的 GitHub 链接替换为匿名版本（anonymous.4open.science 或 "Code available upon acceptance"）

---

### Phase 2: 并行调研（与 Phase 1 同时进行）

#### 2.1 HCAPO ALFWorld 数据查找
- 搜索 HCAPO (Hindsight Credit Assignment for Process Optimization) 论文中 ALFWorld 的具体实验结果（+13.8% 的出处和具体设置）
- 搜索 GiGPO/Turn-PPO 在 ALFWorld 上的报告数字（如果有）
- 用我们的诊断框架分析 HCAPO 的设计：它解决了 M1、M2、还是两者？预测其在什么条件下优于/劣于 episode-level GRPO
- 结果写入 Discussion 的 HCAPO 分析预测段

#### 2.2 Binary Reward 实验最终分析
- 服务器 bjb1 上 `/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl` 应有 600 条记录（3 iterations）
- 分析 iteration 3 的结果（iteration 1: 200 records, iteration 2: 200 records all-zero, iteration 3: ?）
- 关键问题：iteration 3 是否有成功 episode？最终 sign-episode correlation 和 action-validity-based credit agreement 分别是多少？
- 结果用于补强 Section 4 的 binary vs penalty reward 对比分析

---

### Phase 3: 编译验证
- 所有修改完成后 `paper_compile_and_check` 验证编译通过、页数 ≤ 8
- 列出所有修改的段落 + 对应的 review issue ID，方便后续 Reviewer 验证

---

### 优先级排序
1. **最高优先级**：Section 4.1 reward description fix + 三指标区分（W2 核心问题）
2. **高优先级**：Contribution 结构调整 + Proposition 2 upper bound + Table 1 标注 + Cell C 重框架化
3. **中优先级**：引用修复 + 新增引用 + 匿名性修复 + Conclusion 缩窄
4. **并行**：HCAPO 调研 + binary reward 分析

完成后汇报所有修改清单 + 编译结果。

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Reinforcement learning from sparse terminal reward has emerged as a promising paradigm for training LLM agents on multi-turn interactive tasks spanning 30--50 interaction steps~\cite{kim2025reflact,digiRL2024,wang2022scienceworld}. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath}, with its critic-free design, has become the algorithm of choice. Yet a gap persists between the promise of RL-driven agent improvement and the reality of training outcomes: recent agent RL systems~\cite{ragen2025,selfguide2026,spear2025,feng2025gigpo} share a common, unscrutinized implementation pattern (decomposing trajectories into fixed-size windows, each optimized independently) and frequently report training instability without diagnosing its root cause.
5	
6	We show this \textit{step-independent} default introduces a fundamental credit assignment pathology. Step-independent training partitions trajectories into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each optimized via GRPO. This produces a two-phase collapse: task success drops to 0\% while action validity initially remains high, then continued training destroys even basic formatting. Standard remedies (dense reward shaping, discount tuning) fail. Concurrent methods that improve per-step credit quality (GiGPO~\cite{feng2025gigpo}, GAGPO~\cite{gagpo2026}, Turn-PPO~\cite{turnppo2026}, HCAPO~\cite{hcapo2026}) address the symptom through improved credit assignment mechanisms, validating that sign correctness is the critical factor. Our contribution is orthogonal: we formally diagnose \textit{why} the default windowing fails.
7	
8	We trace the collapse to two mechanisms at different layers. \textit{\textbf{Policy information loss}} (M1) operates at the inference layer: each window lacks access to earlier observations, imposing a constant performance ceiling. \textit{\textbf{Advantage sign misattribution}} (M2) operates at the training layer: under binary reward, the 
…[truncated 1881 chars]

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Step-independent training fails through two mechanisms: information loss imposes a constant performance ceiling while sign misattribution causes cumulative degradation, with the majority of gradient updates carrying incorrect sign. Episode-level GRPO addresses both failure modes, achieving substantial OOD improvement at both 1.7B and 8B scales, though only a minority of seeds achieve statistically significant generalization. Sign errors are more destructive than magnitude imprecision; this finding applies to any agent RL method combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization.
5

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Problem Formulation and GRPO}
5	\label{sec:problem_formulation}
6	
7	We model the multi-turn agent as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) where the policy $\pi_\phi(a_t \mid h_t)$ generates actions conditioned on interaction history $h_t = (o_1, a_1, \ldots, o_t)$. Episodes of $T$ steps terminate with binary reward $R \in \{0, r_+\}$. GRPO~\cite{shao2024deepseekmath} samples $K$ rollouts per task and computes group-relative advantages:
8	\begin{align}
9	\hat{A}_k &= \frac{R_k - \mu}{\sigma + \epsilon}, \notag \\
10	\mu &= \frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K R_j, \quad \sigma = \sqrt{\frac{1}{K}\sum_{j=1}^K (R_j - \mu)^2}
11	\label{eq:grpo_advantage}
12	\end{align}
13	GRPO is episode-level by design: each rollout receives one return and one advantage, with no inherent temporal discounting.
14	
15	\subsection{Step-Independent Windowing}
16	\label{sec:step_independent}
17	
18	Agent RL frameworks must reconcile GRPO's episode-level design with the memory constraints of long trajectories. RAGEN~\cite{ragen2025} introduces \textbf{step-independent training}: the trajectory is partitioned into non-overlapping $h$-step windows, each receiving a discounted return:
19	\begin{equation}
20	R_k = \gamma^{(N - k)} \cdot R_{\text{episode}}
21	\label{eq:step_return}
22	\end{equation}
23	where $N = \lceil T/h \rceil$ is the number of windows. Each window is optimized as an independent GRPO sample. This reduces sequence length from $O(T)$ to $O(h)$ and multiplies the effective batch size, but structurally alters credit assignment.
24	
25	\begin{figure*}[t]
26	\centering
27	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{fig_overview.pdf}
28	\caption{Step-independent windowing (left) partitions the trajectory into fixed windows, each optimized independently. This introduces two failure mechanisms: M1 (information loss) truncates context at window boundaries, and M2 (sign misattribution) assigns all windows the same advantage sign base
…[truncated 3323 chars]

Episode-level fix: necessary but not sufficient\n\n**要求**：把 Contribution 3 合并到 Contribution 2 中。Episode-level GRPO 不是新方法（它是 GRPO 原始设计），不能作为独立贡献。合并后只保留 2 个 Contribution。\n\nContribution 2 合并后大意（精确措辞你来写，保持学术风格）：\n\"We validate this diagnosis through a 2×2 factorial experiment that isolates each mechanism, direct sign measurement confirms the theoretical prediction, and preliminary cross-scale (8B) and cross-environment (ScienceWorld) evidence supports generality. Episode-level training (reverting to GRPO's original design) addresses both mechanisms, but a systematic 11-seed evaluation reveals seed-dependent generalization: 73% avoid collapse while only 36% achieve significant OOD improvement.\"\n\n#### 2b. 修正 \"majority\" claim\n当前 introduction.tex line 8 有：\"Our theoretical analysis shows that the majority of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign\"\n\n在 penalty reward 下 credit agreement 是 52%（约一半），不是 majority。改为：\"Our theoretical analysis shows that a substantial fraction of gradient updates for locally correct actions carry incorrect sign\"\n\n注意：这个 claim 在 conclusion.tex 中也有类似表述，一并修改。\n\n#### 2c. 8B 加 \"preliminary\"\n当前 introduction.tex line 8 有 \"with effects persisting at 8B scale\" 或类似。8B 是单 seed 结果，必须加 \"preliminary\" 修饰。改为 \"with preliminary evidence of persistence at 8B scale\" 或类似。\n\n### 3. Conclusion (`conclusion.tex`)\n\n- \"the majority of gradient updates carrying incorrect sign\" → \"a substantial fraction of gradient updates carrying incorrect sign\"\n- \"applies to any agent RL method\" → 缩窄为 \"applies to methods combining step-level windowing, sparse binary reward, and group-relative normalization\"（注意：当前可能已经包含这些条件，检查是否需要调整）\n- \"achieving substantial OOD improvement at both 1.7B and 8B scales\" → 加 \"preliminary\" 修饰 8B：\"achieving substantial OOD improvement at 1.7B scale, with preliminary single-seed evidence at 8B\"\n- \"addresses both failure modes\" 保持（不用 \"eliminates\"）\n\n### 验证\n修改完成后：\n1. 数一下 abstract 的 word count，确认 ≤ 200\n2. 确认 introduction 只有 2 个 \\item\n3. 确认所有 \"majority\" 相关的 claim 已修正\n4. 确认 8B 处都有 \"preliminary\" 修饰",

{
  "task_type": "paper:round3_discussion",
  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：Round 3 审稿修复 — Discussion 新增 M1 稳定化假设段\n\n论文路径：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/docs/paper/`\n\n先 Read `/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/paper-writing-norms.skill.md` 了解写作规范。\n\n### 背景\n在 2×2 factorial 实验中：\n- Cell A (h=2, step-level advantage): M1+M2 both active → gradual collapse to 0%\n- Cell B (h=2, episode-level advantage): M1 only → bounded learning (18.75% ID, 5.97% OOD)\n- Cell C (full trajectory, step-level advantage): M2 only → **NaN instability**（训练崩溃，不同于 Cell A 的渐进退化）\n- Cell D (full trajectory, episode-level advantage): neither → 34.38% ID, 17.16% OOD\n\nCell C 的 NaN instability 与 Cell A 的 gradual collapse 是质性不同的失败模式。这暗示 M1-M2 可能存在非加性交互。\n\n### 修改内容\n\nRead `discussion.tex`，在 \"Generality and mechanism interaction\" 段落之后（或作为新段落），添加一个 **M1 稳定化假设** 段。\n\n新段落内容要点（用 hypothesis/suggests 措辞，不过度声明）：\n\n\\paragraph{Non-additive mechanism interaction.}\nCell C (full trajectory + step-level advantage, isolating M2) produces NaN training instability rather than Cell A's gradual collapse. This qualitative difference suggests that information loss (M1) may have an unintended stabilizing effect: windowing restricts the gradient computation to short sequences, limiting gradient variance. Removing M1 while retaining M2 may release unstable gradient signals that full-trajectory optimization amplifies. If confirmed, this would limit the applicability of the additive separation assumed by the factorial design---the two mechanisms may interact non-linearly, with M1 partially masking M2's destabilizing potential. We leave systematic investigation of this interaction to future work.\n\n**注意**：\n- 措辞用 \"suggests\"、\"may\"、\"if confirmed\" 等 hedging language\n- 不要过度声明，这是基于一个 Cell 的观察\n- 保持简短（4-5 句）\n- 不要与现有的 \"Generality and mechanism interaction\" 段落内容重复\n\n### 页面约束\n论文主文本必须 ≤ 8 页（EMNLP 2026）。当前主文本约 7.6 页。新增段落会增加约 0.1-0.15 页。如果你判断加完后可能超页，请在新段落中进一步精简措辞。\n\n### 验证\n修改完成后 Read discussion.tex 确认新段落已正确添加，且与上下文连贯。",
  "tool_groups": "['knowledge']"
}

Removing M1 while retaining M2 may release unstable gradient signals that full-trajectory optimization amplifies. If confirmed, this would limit the applicability of the additive separation assumed by the factorial design---the two mechanisms may interact non-linearly, with M1 partially masking M2's destabilizing potential. We leave systematic investigation of this interaction to future work.\n\n**注意**：\n- 措辞用 \"suggests\"、\"may\"、\"if confirmed\" 等 hedging language\n- 不要过度声明，这是基于一个 Cell 的观察\n- 保持简短（4-5 句）\n- 不要与现有的 \"Generality and mechanism interaction\" 段落内容重复\n\n### 页面约束\n论文主文本必须 ≤ 8 页（EMNLP 2026）。当前主文本约 7.6 页。新增段落会增加约 0.1-0.15 页。如果你判断加完后可能超页，请在新段落中进一步精简措辞。\n\n### 验证\n修改完成后 Read discussion.tex 确认新段落已正确添加，且与上下文连贯。",
    "tool_groups": "['knowledge']"
  }
}

{
  "task_type": "research:hcapo_alfworld_analysis",
  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：HCAPO ALFWorld 数据查找 + 竞品分析\n\n### 背景\n我们的论文研究 step-independent agent RL 的 credit assignment 失败。我们提出两个机制：\n- M1 (Policy Information Loss): 窗口化截断上下文，限制性能上界\n- M2 (Advantage Sign Misattribution): 优势函数符号反映 episode 结果而非 local action quality\n\nEpisode-level GRPO（用完整 trajectory 作为单个优化样本）解决了两个机制。\n\n### 搜索任务\n\n#### 1. HCAPO (Hindsight Credit Assignment for Process Optimization)\n用 WebSearch 搜索以下信息：\n- HCAPO 论文全文或 arXiv 页面\n- 关键词：\"HCAPO ALFWorld\"、\"Hindsight Credit Assignment Process Optimization\"、\"HCAPO agent RL\"\n- 找到 ALFWorld 上的具体实验结果（他们报告的 +13.8% 提升的具体设置：baseline 是什么、模型大小、评估集、成功率数值）\n- HCAPO 的方法核心：它怎么做 credit assignment？是 per-step 还是 per-turn？用了什么 advantage 估计？\n\n#### 2. GiGPO 和 Turn-PPO 在 ALFWorld 上的结果\n- 搜索 \"GiGPO ALFWorld\" — 是否有 ALFWorld 实验？报告了什么数值？\n- 搜索 \"Turn-PPO ALFWorld\" — 是否有 ALFWorld 实验？\n- 如果没有 ALFWorld 结果，记录\"未在 ALFWorld 上评估\"\n\n#### 3. 用我们的框架分析 HCAPO\n根据搜索到的信息，分析：\n- HCAPO 解决了 M1、M2、还是两者？\n  - 如果 HCAPO 保留了 windowing 但改进了 advantage 计算 → 它主要解决 M2\n  - 如果 HCAPO 使用了 full trajectory context → 它同时解决 M1\n- 预测：在什么条件下 HCAPO 优于/劣于 episode-level GRPO？\n  - 如果 HCAPO 有 per-step credit 但用了更好的 advantage → 可能在长 episode 上优于 episode-level（更精细的 credit）\n  - 如果 HCAPO 仍受 windowing 限制 → 在 information loss 严重的场景下劣于 episode-level\n\n### 输出\n将所有发现写入 artifacts 文件：\n`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/hcapo_analysis.md`\n\n格式：\n```\n# HCAPO / 竞品 ALFWorld 分析\n\n## HCAPO\n- 方法描述：...\n- ALFWorld 结果：...（数值、baseline、设置）\n- 在我们框架下的分析：解决 M1/M2/两者？\n- 预测：优于/劣于 episode-level GRPO 的条件\n\n## GiGPO\n- ALFWorld 结果：有/无\n\n## Turn-PPO\n- ALFWorld 结果：有/无\n\n## 建议写入 Discussion 的段落草稿\n（基于分析写一段 LaTeX 文本，可直接用于 discussion.tex）\n```",
  "tools": "['WebSearch', 'WebFetch']",
  "tool_groups": "['knowledge']"
}

SSH 到 bjb1 检查数据完整性\n```bash\n# 检查总行数\nwc -l /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl\n\n# 检查 iteration 分布\ncat /root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\niters = {}\nfor line in sys.stdin:\n    d = json.loads(line)\n    it = d.get('iteration', 'unknown')\n    iters[it] = iters.get(it, 0) + 1\nfor k, v in sorted(iters.items()):\n    print(f'Iteration {k}: {v} records')\n\"\n```\n\n#### 2. 分析 Iteration 3（如果有数据）\n```python\nimport json\n\nrecords = []\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl') as f:\n    for line in f:\n        d = json.loads(line)\n        if d.get('iteration') == 3:  # 或最后一个 iteration\n            records.append(d)\n\n# 关键指标：\n# 1. 多少 episode 成功 vs 失败\n# 2. 非零优势窗口中 sign-episode agreement rate\n# 3. 零优势窗口比例（reward saturation）\n# 4. Action validity based credit agreement（用 action_is_valid 字段）\n```\n\n#### 3. 汇总三个 iteration 的完整统计\n对每个 iteration 计算：\n- 总 group 数\n- 成功 episode 数 / 失败 episode 数\n- 非零优势窗口数 / 零优势窗口数\n- Sign-episode agreement rate（仅非零优势窗口）\n- Credit agreement rate（基于 action validity，如果有该字段）\n\n#### 4. 输出\n将分析结果写入：\n`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_reward_final_analysis.md`\n\n格式：\n```\n# Binary Reward 实验最终分析\n\n## 数据完整性\n- 总记录数：X\n- Iteration 分布：...\n\n## Per-Iteration 统计\n| Metric | Iter 1 | Iter 2 | Iter 3 |\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|\n| Groups | ... | ... | ... |\n| Success episodes | ... | ... | ... |\n| Non-zero adv windows | ... | ... | ... |\n| Zero adv windows (%) | ... | ... | ... |\n| Sign-episode agreement | ... | ... | ... |\n\n## 关键发现\n- ...\n\n## 论文使用建议\n- 哪些数值可以更新到 Section 4.4\n- 是否改变了 \"100% sign-episode correlation\" 的 claim\n```\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：bjb1（应该在 SSH config 中）\n- 实验 PID: 14928（可能已完成）\n- 数据路径：/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl",

Action validity based credit agreement（用 action_is_valid 字段）\n```\n\n#### 3. 汇总三个 iteration 的完整统计\n对每个 iteration 计算：\n- 总 group 数\n- 成功 episode 数 / 失败 episode 数\n- 非零优势窗口数 / 零优势窗口数\n- Sign-episode agreement rate（仅非零优势窗口）\n- Credit agreement rate（基于 action validity，如果有该字段）\n\n#### 4. 输出\n将分析结果写入：\n`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/binary_reward_final_analysis.md`\n\n格式：\n```\n# Binary Reward 实验最终分析\n\n## 数据完整性\n- 总记录数：X\n- Iteration 分布：...\n\n## Per-Iteration 统计\n| Metric | Iter 1 | Iter 2 | Iter 3 |\n|--------|--------|--------|--------|\n| Groups | ... | ... | ... |\n| Success episodes | ... | ... | ... |\n| Non-zero adv windows | ... | ... | ... |\n| Zero adv windows (%) | ... | ... | ... |\n| Sign-episode agreement | ... | ... | ... |\n\n## 关键发现\n- ...\n\n## 论文使用建议\n- 哪些数值可以更新到 Section 4.4\n- 是否改变了 \"100% sign-episode correlation\" 的 claim\n```\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：bjb1（应该在 SSH config 中）\n- 实验 PID: 14928（可能已完成）\n- 数据路径：/root/autodl-tmp/binary_credit_adv_log.jsonl",

{
  "task_type": "research:hcapo_alfworld",
  "project": "cost-verify-agent-rl",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：HCAPO ALFWorld 数据查找 + 诊断框架分析\n\n### 背景\n我们的论文研究 step-independent agent RL 中的 sign misattribution 问题。我们诊断了两个机制：\n- M1 (Policy Information Loss)：窗口化截断上下文，限制策略能力\n- M2 (Advantage Sign Misattribution)：binary reward 下 advantage sign 反映 episode outcome 而非 local action quality\n\nHCAPO (Hindsight Credit Assignment for Process Optimization) 是一篇声称通过 hindsight credit 解决 multi-turn agent RL 问题的论文。\n\n### 任务\n\n#### 1. 搜索 HCAPO 论文\n- WebSearch \"HCAPO Hindsight Credit Assignment Process Optimization\"\n- 找到论文 PDF/HTML，获取以下信息：\n  - ALFWorld 的具体实验结果（据说有 +13.8% improvement，需确认具体设置和 baseline）\n  - 模型大小、训练方法、reward 设计\n  - 它的 credit assignment 方法具体是什么\n\n#### 2. 搜索 GiGPO 和 Turn-PPO 在 ALFWorld 上的结果\n- WebSearch \"GiGPO ALFWorld\" 和 \"Turn-PPO ALFWorld\"\n- 如果有报告数字，记录下来\n\n#### 3. 用我们的诊断框架分析 HCAPO\n- HCAPO 解决了 M1、M2、还是两者？\n  - 如果它仍然用窗口化但改进了 credit assignment → 可能解决 M2 但保留 M1\n  - 如果它用 full trajectory → 可能同时解决 M1 和 M2\n- 预测：在什么条件下 HCAPO 优于/劣于 episode-level GRPO？\n  - 如果 HCAPO 有 per-step credit → 可能在长序列上比 uniform episode-level advantage 更高效\n  - 但如果 per-step credit 估计不准确 → 可能引入新的 noise\n\n#### 4. 其他竞品\n搜索是否有其他论文在 ALFWorld 上报告了 RL 训练结果（不是 SFT/prompting，是 RL fine-tuning），记录结果\n\n### 产出\n将所有发现写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_1/projects/cost-verify-agent-rl/artifacts/hcapo_analysis.md`\n\n格式：\n```markdown\n# HCAPO ALFWorld Analysis\n\n## HCAPO Results\n- ALFWorld 具体数字：...\n- 模型/方法/baseline：...\n- Credit assignment 方法描述：...\n\n## GiGPO / Turn-PPO ALFWorld Results\n- ...\n\n## Diagnostic Framework Analysis\n- M1 addressed? ...\n- M2 addressed? ...\n- Predicted advantages over episode-level GRPO: ...\n- Predicted limitations: ...\n\n## Other ALFWorld RL Results\n- ...\n```",
  "tools": "['WebSearch', 'WebFetch']",
  "tool_groups": "['knowledge']"
}
